Which pages to read for what you are building

Find your pages

Pick what you are building. Every page lists its endpoints at the top; links can be shared (for example #slots/post-wagers-instant opens that endpoint on the Slots page). Press / to search every endpoint.

You are buildingKeyRead
A sportsbook, crash, slots or scratch card game serverGame key, bound to your game, plus a player tokenYour game's page: Sportsbook, Crash, Slots or Scratch Cards. It lists everything you call, in order. The full rules are in Player Sessions and the Wager API.
The kadi game serverFull keyKadi and its pages (game wallets, game bets, competitions)

GMS (back office)Full keyBack office, Catalog & Products, Bet Shops and Finance Reporting

Every integration

Authentication (base URL, API key header, encrypted ids, idempotency, rate limits and conventions), API Keys (scopes) and Error Responses.

Base URL and format

All endpoints on this page are under https://api.kadi-kings.co.ke/api/v1 and speak JSON. Send Accept: application/json on every request: without it, a validation failure redirects (302) instead of returning 422 JSON. Send Content-Type: application/json with a body.

X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

API Key Authentication

Every endpoint needs an active API key in the X-API-KEY header. A missing, unknown or deactivated key returns:

HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized

{"message": "Invalid or missing API key"}

A shop key (issued to a bet shop's system under Bet Shops) works only on the /api/v1/shop/* routes; anywhere else it gets 403 {"message": "A shop API key can only be used on shop routes"}. A site key (the kadi site) and a game key (a game server) work only on the routes listed for their scope (see API Keys); anywhere else they get 403 {"message": "This API key cannot use this route", "code": "route_not_allowed"}. A full key works everywhere except the shop routes. Existing keys are full keys and are unchanged.

Actions that change money record which key made them (as api_key:{id}), for example in resolved_by, created_by and wallet adjustments.

Encrypted Identifiers

Ids in URL paths ({encryptedIdentifier}) are encrypted with OpenSSL AES-128-CBC (the cipher is set on the server by OPENSSL_METHOD): a random 16-byte IV followed by the ciphertext, URL-safe base64 encoded. Each call produces a different string for the same id; the server decrypts them before the request reaches the endpoint. Use POST /api/v1/encrypt below to build them.

A value that cannot be decrypted returns 400 {"message": "Invalid identifier."}. Customer endpoints match the decrypted value against the customer id or account number, so an encrypted account number works too.

Idempotency

Endpoints tagged Idempotent store their response for 24 hours under an idempotency key and replay it for a repeat of the same request: the same status and body, without running the action again.

  • Send your own Idempotency-Key header (up to 64 characters; longer returns 400): one new key per user action, and the same key when retrying that action after a timeout.
  • Reusing a key with a different body returns 409 {"message": "Idempotency-Key reused with different request body"}.
  • Keys are kept per API key: another integration that happens to send the same key value never gets your response or a 409 because of it.
  • Without a header, the key is made from the API key, endpoint and body. Two identical requests within 24 hours (for example two withdrawals of the same amount for the same customer) are then treated as one: the second gets the first response and nothing happens. Client error responses (4xx) are stored too, so an identical retry of a refused request gets the same refusal for 24 hours; server errors (5xx) are not, so a retry after one runs again. Always send a fresh key for a new action.
Idempotency-Key: 5f0c2b4e-8a1d-4f7e-9c33-1d2e3f4a5b6c

Rate Limits

Limits are per API key: every request made with one key (for example everyone working through GMS) shares that key's budget, and different keys never share one. A request with a missing or unknown key is limited per client IP address. A server-side key can be given a bigger budget with php artisan apikey:rate-limit {key id or name} {1-20}, which multiplies every limit below for that key (the bet shop limits under Shop System are not multiplied). Over the limit returns 429 {"message": "Too Many Attempts."} with a Retry-After header (seconds).

LimiterLimitApplies to
write30/minEndpoints tagged 30/min: most creates and money-moving writes
financial5/minEndpoints tagged 5/min: STK Push, loads, M-Pesa withdrawals, referral withdrawals
stats20/minAll /stats/* and /finance/* reports
callback100/minSafaricom callbacks (not documented here)

Endpoints without a tag have no limit.

Dates, money and statuses

  • Record timestamps (created_at, updated_at) are UTC ISO 8601, e.g. 2026-09-20T07:15:00.000000Z. Fields built for display (e.g. created_at in played-game lists, trans_time) are Nairobi time Y-m-d H:i:s. Newer endpoints return ISO 8601 with the Nairobi offset (+03:00).
  • Money is KES. Amounts stored on a record come back as strings with 2 decimals ("1500.00"); totals and newer endpoints return numbers.
  • Transaction status: 1 pending, 2 completed, 3 failed. Withdrawal disburse: 1 pending, 2 sent to M-Pesa, 3 failed. Deposit status: 0 unmatched, 1 pending, 2 completed, 4 refunded.

API Key
200 OK 500 Server Error

Request Body

identifierstring|integerRequiredThe plain id or account number

Example Request

POST /api/v1/encrypt HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "identifier": 42
}

Response (200)

{
  "decrypted_id": 42,
  "encrypted_id": "q3n8Xk2vH7Qm0bY4c1Pz9w8R2k6T5sVdLx0aF3yUe4c"
}

Response (500: encryption failed)

{
  "error": "openssl_encrypt(): ..."
}

Also returns 401 (API key): see Error Responses.

  "decrypted_id": "42",
  "encrypted_id": "q3n8Xk2vH7Qm0bY4c1Pz9w8R2k6T5sVdLx0aF3yUe4c"
}

Response (500: decryption failed)

{
  "error": "..."
}

A value that is not valid ciphertext returns decrypted_id: false or a 500, depending on where it fails.

Also returns 401 (API key): see Error Responses.

Key scopes. Every key has a scope: full (GMS and the kadi game server: every route except the shop system's, as every key had before scopes existed), site (the kadi site: only the player routes kadi uses), game (a game server, bound to one game: only that game's routes) or shop (a bet shop's system, issued under Bet Shops). Site and game keys start with site_ and game_. Full and site keys are made on the server (php artisan apiKey:generate {name} --scope=site); game keys can also be issued below. A key is shown once, when it is issued, and never again. A revoked key gets 401 straight away.
GET/api/v1/api-keysList API keys (never the key itself)▶
API Key

Description

Every key of every scope, newest first.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
scopestringOptionalfull, shop, site or game
gamestringOptionalA game code: only that game's keys
activebooleanOptional1 active only, 0 revoked only
pageintegerOptionalDefault 1
per_pageintegerOptional1 to 200, default 50

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {"id": 12, "name": "crash-server", "scope": "game", "game": "crash", "bet_shop_no": null, "is_active": true, "rate_limit_multiplier": 1,
       "revoked_at": null, "revoked_by": null, "created_at": "2026-10-29T19:00:00+03:00"}
    ],
    "pagination": {"page": 1, "per_page": 50, "total": 1, "last_page": 1}
  }
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 422 (unknown scope): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/games/{code}/keysIssue a game server key▶
API Key30/min

Description

Issues a game key bound to the game. data.key is shown only in this response: store it in the game server's configuration straight away. Not idempotent on purpose: a stored response would keep the key in the database for 24 hours. A retired game gets no new keys.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
namestringRequired3 to 255 characters, e.g. crash-server-1

Response (201)

{"success": true, "message": "Key issued. It is shown only once.", "data": {"id": 12, "name": "crash-server-1", "scope": "game", "game": "crash", "bet_shop_no": null, "is_active": true, "rate_limit_multiplier": 1, "revoked_at": null, "revoked_by": null, "created_at": "2026-10-29T19:00:00+03:00", "key": "game_..."}}

Response (404 / 409)

{"success": false, "message": "Game not found"}
{"success": false, "code": "game_retired", "message": "The game is retired"}

Also returns 401 (API key), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/api-keys/{encryptedIdentifier}/revokeRevoke any API key▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min

Description

Stops a key of any scope working at once. Revoking an already revoked key changes nothing and returns it with who revoked it first. A key cannot revoke itself (409 own_key), so GMS cannot lock itself out by mistake.

Response (200)

{"success": true, "message": "Key revoked", "data": {"id": 12, "name": "crash-server-1", "scope": "game", "game": "crash", "bet_shop_no": null, "is_active": false, "rate_limit_multiplier": 1, "revoked_at": "2026-10-29T20:00:00+03:00", "revoked_by": "api_key:2", "created_at": "2026-10-29T19:00:00+03:00"}}

Response (404 / 409)

{"success": false, "message": "API key not found"}
{"success": false, "code": "own_key", "message": "A key cannot revoke itself"}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

How a game is launched. Players log in only on kadi; the API never takes a player's password. When a player clicks a game, kadi (site key) asks for a launch token and sends the player to the game's launch_url with it (an auto-submitted form, token field). The game server (its game key) redeems the token once for a player token, good for that player in that game only, for 12 hours. The game sends the player token as Authorization: Bearer … on the player routes, together with its own X-API-KEY. When the player token expires, the player relaunches from kadi.
kadi  ── POST /customers/{id}/launch-tokens {game} ──▶ API ── {token, launch_url} ──▶ kadi
kadi  ── form POST {launch_url} token=… ──▶ game
game  ── POST /launch-tokens/redeem {token} ──▶ API ── {player_token, customer, balance} ──▶ game
POST/api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/launch-tokensLaunch a game for a logged-in player (kadi)▶
API KeyEncrypted ID30/min

Description

Issues a single-use launch token for this player and game, valid for 120 seconds. Call it only for a player kadi has logged in: the API trusts the site key to vouch for them. The player must be active (status 1) and the game active with a launch URL. Not idempotent on purpose: a stored response would replay a live token.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
gamestringRequiredA game code from GET /api/v1/games, e.g. crash
ipstringOptionalThe player's IP address as kadi saw it (kept for audit)

Response (201)

{"success": true, "message": "Launch token issued. It works once, within its expiry.", "data": {"token": "Xq3…40 characters", "expires_at": "2026-10-30T12:02:00+03:00", "launch_url": "https://crash.kadikings.co.ke/launch"}}

Response (403 / 404)

{"success": false, "message": "This player cannot play", "code": "player_inactive"}
{"success": false, "message": "This game cannot be launched now", "code": "game_unavailable"}
{"success": false, "message": "Customer not found"}
{"success": false, "message": "Game not found"}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/launch-tokens/redeemStart a player session from a launch token (game server)▶
API Key30/min

Description

Game keys only. Uses the launch token up and returns a player token for this game, the player and their balance. A token works once, only for the game it was issued for, and only before it expires. Every bad token (unknown, expired, already used, another game's) gets the same 401, and a token sent by the wrong game is not used up. The player and the game are checked again here. If the call times out, do not retry: the token may already be used, so send the player back to kadi to relaunch. data.player_token is shown only in this response.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
tokenstringRequiredThe 40-character launch token the game received from kadi

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Player session started",
  "data": {
    "player_token": "15|Hn0…",
    "expires_at": "2026-10-31T00:00:00+03:00",
    "customer": {"id": 2041, "player_no": "48213907", "account_no": "KK-6AA8B1DAAF392", "display_name": "Jane W.", "status": 1},
    "balance": {"balance": 250.5, "locked_bonus": 0, "wallet_version": 37}
  }
}

Response (401 / 403)

{"success": false, "message": "Invalid or expired launch token", "code": "invalid_launch_token"}
{"message": "This route needs a game API key", "code": "game_key_required"}
{"success": false, "message": "This player cannot play", "code": "player_inactive"}
{"success": false, "message": "This game cannot be launched now", "code": "game_unavailable"}

display_name is the player's first name and last initial, or Player 1234 (last four digits of the player number) when there is no name: safe for a public feed. The API never gives a game the player's phone, email, national ID or full name. balance may be negative after a settlement is reversed; locked_bonus is signup bonus that cannot be withdrawn yet but may be staked. Also returns 401 (API key), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

GET/api/v1/meThe player behind a player token (game server)▶
API Key

Description

Game key plus Authorization: Bearer {player_token}. The same player shape as the redeem response.

Response (200)

{"success": true, "data": {"id": 2041, "player_no": "48213907", "account_no": "KK-6AA8B1DAAF392", "display_name": "Jane W.", "status": 1}}

Response (401 / 403)

{"message": "Invalid or expired player token", "code": "invalid_player_token"}
{"message": "This player cannot play", "code": "player_inactive"}
{"message": "This route needs a game API key", "code": "game_key_required"}

A missing, unknown or expired token, or one issued for another game, all get the same 401: send the player back to kadi to relaunch. Also returns 401 (API key): see Error Responses.

GET/api/v1/me/balanceThe player's wallet balance (game server)▶
API Key

Description

Game key plus player token. The one wallet the player has across every game. wallet_version goes up on every change, so a game can tell a stale balance from a fresh one. locked_bonus is signup bonus that may be staked but not withdrawn; it is already inside balance. A player without a wallet gets zeros.

Response (200)

{"success": true, "data": {"balance": 250.5, "locked_bonus": 0, "wallet_version": 37}}

Errors as for GET /api/v1/me. Also returns 401 (API key): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/player-tokens/revokeEnd a player's game sessions (kadi)▶
API KeyEncrypted ID30/min

Description

Call it when the player logs out of kadi. Every game the player is in gets 401 invalid_player_token on its next call. Send game to end only that game's sessions. Repeating the call is harmless (revoked is 0). The API also ends every session by itself when a customer's status changes away from 1 or the customer is deleted; reactivating the customer does not bring old sessions back.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
gamestringOptionalA game code: only that game's sessions

Response (200)

{"success": true, "message": "Player sessions ended", "data": {"revoked": 2}}

Response (404)

{"success": false, "message": "Customer not found"}
{"success": false, "message": "Game not found"}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

Building a game?

Each game page lists, in order, exactly the endpoints that game calls, with notes for that game and its own codes in the examples: Sportsbook, Crash, Slots, Scratch Cards. This page is the full reference they are built from.

Wagers (house-banked games). For a game where the house is the other side of every bet (sportsbook, crash, slots, scratch cards). One wallet per player across every game: POST /api/v1/wagers moves the stake into the bet's own escrow, a settlement pays the escrow out to the house or the player, and the house wallet may go negative (it is owner capital). A bet is identified by product + reference, a reference of your choosing (up to 100 characters). 
place  ──▶ open ──▶ settle v1 (won | lost | void) ──▶ reverse ──▶ open ──▶ settle v2 …
                    └─▶ admin void (GMS, a stuck open bet)
Life cycle. Place (game key plus player token) debits the stake. Settle (game key) is versioned: each settlement sends the next version, and sending the same version with the same values again is a harmless replay. Reverse takes a settlement back exactly and reopens the bet, which can take a player below zero (the debt is repaid from the next credits, and blocks stakes and withdrawals until then). Rounds settle or void every bet of a round in one call. Admin void (GMS) refunds a bet that is stuck open.

Integration pattern.
  1. Session. Redeem the launch token, keep the customer_id and player token in the game's session, never a password. An expired player token means the player relaunches from kadi.
  2. Place is synchronous. Write the bet locally as pending and commit; call POST /wagers with its reference. On 201 or 200 mark it open. On any 4xx other than 429 mark it rejected (403 product_not_active, player_excluded, player_inactive; 409 round_closed, wager_voided, reference_conflict; any 422). Retry only timeouts, 429 and 5xx: on those retry with the same reference, then GET /wagers/{reference}?product=. If the bet is still unknown, send a void settle for that reference (a tombstone, which makes the reference unusable) and mark the bet void. Count pending bets as exposure.
  3. Settle is asynchronous, through an outbox. In the same local transaction that settles the bet, insert an outbox row (reference, version, operation, payload). A queued job delivers the rows for one bet in order, with retries. 409 version_out_of_order: wait and retry later, an earlier version has not arrived. A replay (replayed: true) counts as delivered. For a per-wager settle, 409 settlement_conflict after a GMS admin void is terminal: stop retrying and report it to your operators. A round call is different: a 409 settlement_conflict or 422 wager_not_in_round refuses the whole call and settles nothing, so it must not be treated as terminal. Report the listed references, remove them from winners and send the call again; the rest of the round is not settled until you do. A 200 can also carry skipped_winners (winners that stopped being open after the call checked them, for example voided by GMS): report those too; they were not paid.
  4. Instant games (slots, scratch cards). When the result is known as the bet is made, call POST /wagers/instant once per spin or card with the stake and the result: there is nothing to settle later and no outbox. On a timeout, 429 or 5xx send the same call again with the same reference (a replay answers 200), then GET /wagers/{reference}?product=; if the bet is still unknown, no money moved and the spin did not happen. A 403 or 422 means the spin was refused and no money moved: show it as not played. A 409 reference_conflict does not mean that: the reference is already in use (the spin went through and was reversed since, or the retry's values differ from the first call's). Look it up with GET /wagers/{reference} and reconcile from there. A scratch card that is bought now and revealed later is a normal place, then a settle when it is revealed. Each slot title, and each scratch card type and price, is its own product; a card's price is its product's min_stake and max_stake.
  5. Never call the wallet while holding a game lock (for example crash's round row). Take the lock, decide, release, then call.
Rounds. Place bets with a round_reference, then end the round with POST /rounds/{reference}/settle (the winners; every other open bet loses) or POST /rounds/{reference}/void (refund all). At most 1,000 winners per call: send larger rounds in batches with final: false (each batch settles its winners and closes the round to new bets, a place answers 409 round_closed) and finish with one call with final: true, sent only after every final: false batch has answered 200: a final call settles every bet still open as lost, including a winner of a batch that has not gone through. A winner that is not a bet of the round answers 422 wager_not_in_round (see step 3: fix the list and resend). Round calls can be repeated safely.

Errors. On the wager and round routes every error is {"success": false, "message": "…", "code": "…", "retryable": false}; retryable is true for 429 rate_limited and 500 server_error. Validation failures are 422 validation_failed with an extra errors object. Codes in use: validation_failed, product_not_allowed, product_not_active, player_inactive, player_excluded, insufficient_funds, stake_below_limit, stake_above_limit, payout_above_limit, round_closed, reference_conflict, wager_voided, wager_not_found, wager_not_in_round, wager_not_open, not_settled, version_out_of_order, settlement_conflict, rate_limited, server_error. The status and meaning of each are in the route blocks below.

Errors that do not have that shape. Three bodies come from the key and token checks before the wager code runs, and they have no success or retryable field. Do not rely on retryable for them: 
401 {"message": "Invalid or missing API key"}
403 {"message": "This API key cannot use this route", "code": "route_not_allowed"}
401 {"message": "Invalid or expired player token", "code": "invalid_player_token"}
Other refusals from the same checks: 403 game_key_required and 403 player_inactive (both with message and code only). The kadi site's history route (GET /customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/wagers) is not a wager-shaped route: its 500 uses the default Laravel error body, not server_error.
POST/api/v1/wagersPlace a bet on a house-banked product (game server)▶
API Key1200/min

Description

Game key plus player token. The stake moves from the player's wallet into the wager's own escrow until the wager is settled. The product must belong to the key's game and be active, and the player must be active and not on a break. A cash bet needs a balance of at least the stake and never a negative balance, and a stake from the product's min_stake to its max_stake (a free bet is not held to them). A free bet (funding free_bet) sends free_bet_amount instead of stake and moves no money. Not covered by the idempotency header: the pair product + reference is the idempotency key. Repeating a place with the same reference and the same payload answers 200 with replayed: true and moves no money, even if the round has closed, the product was suspended or the player went on a break since; the same reference with a different payload answers 409 reference_conflict. A round_reference puts the bet in a round, created on first use.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
productstringRequiredA product code of the key's game
referencestringRequiredYour id for this bet (up to 100 characters), unique per product
fundingstringRequiredcash or free_bet
stakenumberRequired for cashGreater than 0 and at most 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals. Omit (or 0) for a free bet
free_bet_amountnumberRequired for a free betGreater than 0 and at most 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals. Not allowed for cash
round_referencestringOptionalThe round this bet belongs to (up to 100 characters)
channelstringOptionalUp to 20 characters, e.g. web
metadataobjectOptionalAnything you want kept with the bet, up to 4 KB of JSON

Response (201; 200 with replayed true)

{"success": true, "data": {"wager": {"reference": "bet-1", "product": "crash_classic", "funding": "cash", "stake": 100, "free_bet_amount": 0, "status": "open", "settlement_version": 0, "round_reference": "round-1", "placed_at": "2026-10-30T10:15:00+00:00"}, "balance": {"balance": 200, "wallet_version": 38}, "replayed": false}}

Errors

Every error is {"success": false, "message": "…", "code": "…", "retryable": false}; retryable is true only for 429 and 5xx.

StatuscodeWhen
403product_not_allowedThe product is unknown, belongs to another game, or is not house-banked (kadi's rake products never take wagers)
403product_not_activeThe product is suspended or retired
403player_inactive / player_excludedThe player cannot play (excluded_until is added for a break)
409round_closedThe round is settled or voided, or a round settlement has closed it
409reference_conflictThe reference was used with a different payload
409wager_voidedThe reference was voided before it was placed
422stake_below_limitThe cash stake is below the product's min_stake (min_stake is added)
422stake_above_limitThe stake is above the product's max_stake
422insufficient_fundsThe balance is below the stake, or negative
422validation_failedThe body is invalid (errors lists the fields)
429rate_limitedThe 1200/min limit is spent (Retry-After header), retryable true
500server_errorUnexpected failure, retryable true; the same place can be sent again

Also returns 401 (API key, or invalid_player_token), 403 (game_key_required, route_not_allowed): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/wagers/instantPlace and settle a bet in one call: a spin or a scratch card (game server)▶
API Key1200/min

Description

Game key plus player token. For a game whose result is known when the bet is made (a slot spin, a scratch card bought and revealed at once): the place and the version 1 settlement happen in one database transaction, so a spin is never left open. Every check of POST /wagers applies (product, player, min_stake, max_stake, balance), plus the settle's max_payout; when any check refuses the bet, no money moves. The money legs and the winnings tax are exactly those of a place followed by a settle, so reports cannot tell the two apart. outcome is won or lost; a void is never instant (a game that cannot finish a spin does not send it). Not covered by the idempotency header: product + reference is the idempotency key. Repeating the call with the same place fields and the same result answers 200 with replayed: true and moves no money; the same reference with other values, a spin that was reversed since, or a reference placed through POST /wagers that is still open or was settled with another result, answers 409 reference_conflict (a POST /wagers bet the game already settled with exactly these values answers as a replay, moving no money). The settlement is an ordinary version 1: reverse it with POST /wagers/{reference}/reverse and version 1 (the bet reopens with its stake in escrow) and settle it again with POST /wagers/{reference}/settle and version 2.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
product, reference, funding, stake, free_bet_amount, round_reference, channel, metadata As POST /wagersThe bet
outcomestringRequiredwon or lost
payoutnumberRequired0 to 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals, as in a settle (a cash win includes the stake). Must be 0 for lost
bonusnumberOptional0 to 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals. Must be 0 for lost

Response (201; 200 with replayed true)

{"success": true, "data": {"wager": {"reference": "spin-81", "product": "slots_fruit_mania", "funding": "cash", "stake": 20, "free_bet_amount": 0, "status": "won", "settlement_version": 1, "round_reference": null, "placed_at": "2026-10-31T10:15:00+00:00"}, "settlement": {"version": 1, "outcome": "won", "payout": 75, "bonus": 0, "tax": 0, "tax_base": 0, "tax_rate": 0, "reason": null, "settled_by": "game", "status": "applied", "settled_at": "2026-10-31T10:15:00+00:00"}, "balance": {"balance": 355, "wallet_version": 52}, "replayed": false}}

Errors

Every error is {"success": false, "message": "…", "code": "…", "retryable": false}; retryable is true only for 429 and 5xx.

StatuscodeWhen
403product_not_allowed / product_not_activeAs POST /wagers
403player_inactive / player_excludedThe player cannot play
409reference_conflictThe reference was used for a different bet or result, the spin was reversed since, or it is an open POST /wagers bet. Money may have moved: look the bet up with GET /wagers/{reference}
409round_closed / wager_voidedAs POST /wagers
422stake_below_limit / stake_above_limitThe cash stake is outside the product's min_stake to max_stake
422payout_above_limitpayout + bonus is above the product's max_payout
422insufficient_fundsThe balance is below the stake, or negative
422validation_failedThe body is invalid, the outcome is void, or a lost bet has a payout or bonus
429rate_limitedThe key's per-minute limit is spent (Retry-After header), retryable true
500server_errorUnexpected failure, retryable true; send the same call again

Also returns 401 (API key, or invalid_player_token), 403 (game_key_required, route_not_allowed): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/wagers/{reference}/settleSettle a bet as won, lost or void (game server)▶
API Key1200/min

Description

Game key only, no player token: settlements happen after the player has left. Not covered by the idempotency header: product + reference + version is the idempotency key. A wager starts at settlement_version 0 and each settlement must send the next version (1, 2, …) while the wager is open. Repeating a version with the same outcome, payout and bonus answers 200 with replayed: true and moves no money (reason is not compared); the same version with other values answers 409 settlement_conflict; a version that skips one, or a wager that is not open, answers 409 version_out_of_order with the wager's current status and settlement_version. payout is the gross amount credited for a win, before bonus and tax: a cash win includes the stake, a free-bet win excludes the voucher. A lost or void settlement must send payout and bonus of 0, and payout + bonus may not exceed the product's max_payout. A cash loss moves the stake to the house, a cash win also pays the house's payout and bonus to the player, a cash void refunds the stake; a free bet moves nothing unless it wins. Winnings tax is withheld from the player's wallet on a win: the rate is the product's, applied to the profit (payout + bonus − stake, never below 0) for a cash bet and to payout + bonus for a free bet. A settlement is never refused for the house's or the player's balance (the house balance may go negative), and it is allowed while the product is suspended. A void for a reference that was never placed answers 201 and records a tombstone, so a later place of that reference is refused with wager_voided; a won or lost for an unknown reference answers 404 wager_not_found.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
productstringRequiredA product code of the key's game
versionintegerRequiredThe wager's settlement_version + 1 (1 for the first settlement)
outcomestringRequiredwon, lost or void
payoutnumberRequired0 to 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals. Must be 0 unless outcome is won
bonusnumberOptional0 to 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals. Must be 0 unless outcome is won
reasonstringOptionalUp to 255 characters, kept with the settlement

Response (200; 201 for a tombstone; 200 with replayed true)

{"success": true, "data": {"wager": {"reference": "bet-1", "product": "crash_classic", "funding": "cash", "stake": 100, "free_bet_amount": 0, "status": "won", "settlement_version": 1, "round_reference": null, "placed_at": "2026-10-30T10:15:00+00:00"}, "settlement": {"version": 1, "outcome": "won", "payout": 250, "bonus": 30, "tax": 36, "tax_base": 180, "tax_rate": 0.2, "reason": null, "settled_by": "game", "status": "applied", "settled_at": "2026-10-30T10:16:00+00:00"}, "replayed": false}}

Errors

Every error is {"success": false, "message": "…", "code": "…", "retryable": false}; retryable is true only for 429 and 5xx.

StatuscodeWhen
403product_not_allowedThe product is unknown, belongs to another game, or is not house-banked (kadi's rake products never take wagers)
404wager_not_foundA won or lost for a reference that was never placed
409settlement_conflictThis version was already settled with other values (or by an admin)
409version_out_of_orderThe version skips one, or the wager is not open; status and settlement_version give its current state
422payout_above_limitpayout + bonus is above the product's max_payout
422validation_failedThe body is invalid, a lost or void settlement has a payout or bonus, or reference is empty or longer than 100 characters
429rate_limitedThe 1200/min limit is spent (Retry-After header), retryable true
500server_errorUnexpected failure, retryable true; the same settlement can be sent again

Also returns 401 (API key), 403 (game_key_required, route_not_allowed): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/wagers/{reference}/reverseTake back a settlement and reopen the bet (game server)▶
API Key1200/min

Description

Game key only, no player token. Not covered by the idempotency header: product + reference + version is the idempotency key. Send the wager's current settlement_version while it is settled (won, lost or void). The settlement's money legs are negated exactly, in this order: winnings tax back to the player, bonus back to the house, payout back to the house, then the stake back into the bet's escrow (from the house after a loss or win, from the player after a void). The settlement becomes reversed and the wager goes back to open with the same settlement_version, so the next settlement sends version + 1. A reversal is never refused for a balance: the player's wallet may go below zero, as may the house's. While a player's balance is negative, new stakes and withdrawals are refused, and the next deposit repays the debt first. A free-bet wager with no money legs just reopens. Repeating a reversal for a version that is already reversed (the wager still open at that version) answers 200 with replayed: true and moves no money (reason is not compared). A version an admin settled cannot be reversed by a game, and a tombstone (a void for a bet that was never placed) cannot be reversed.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
productstringRequiredA product code of the key's game
versionintegerRequiredThe wager's current settlement_version
reasonstringRequiredUp to 255 characters, kept with the reversed settlement

Response (200; 200 with replayed true)

{"success": true, "data": {"wager": {"reference": "bet-1", "product": "crash_classic", "funding": "cash", "stake": 100, "free_bet_amount": 0, "status": "open", "settlement_version": 1, "round_reference": null, "placed_at": "2026-10-30T10:15:00+00:00"}, "settlement": {"version": 1, "outcome": "won", "payout": 250, "bonus": 30, "tax": 36, "tax_base": 180, "tax_rate": 0.2, "reason": null, "settled_by": "game", "status": "reversed", "settled_at": "2026-10-30T10:16:00+00:00"}, "replayed": false}}

Errors

Every error is {"success": false, "message": "…", "code": "…", "retryable": false}; retryable is true only for 429 and 5xx.

StatuscodeWhen
403product_not_allowedThe product is unknown, belongs to another game, or is not house-banked (kadi's rake products never take wagers)
404wager_not_foundNo wager has that reference
409not_settledThe wager is open and was never settled
409version_out_of_orderversion is not the wager's current settlement; status and settlement_version give its current state
409settlement_conflictAn admin settled this version
409wager_voidedThe reference is a tombstone
422validation_failedThe body is invalid, or reference is empty or longer than 100 characters
429rate_limitedThe 1200/min limit is spent (Retry-After header), retryable true
500server_errorUnexpected failure, retryable true; the same reversal can be sent again

Also returns 401 (API key), 403 (game_key_required, route_not_allowed): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/rounds/{reference}/settleSettle a round's winners, and every other bet as lost (game server)▶
API Key1200/min

Description

Game key only, no player token. {reference} is the round_reference the bets were placed with (up to 100 characters). Settles each listed winner as won at version 1 with its payout and bonus (money, tax and legs exactly as a single won settlement), then, when final is true (the default), every other open bet of the round as lost, and marks the round settled. Send at most 1,000 winners per call: for a larger round send the winners in batches with final: false, which settles only the listed winners and closes the round to new bets (a place into it answers 409 round_closed) while it stays open; then a last call with final: true (its winners may be empty) loses the rest. Send the final call only after every final: false batch has answered 200: it settles every bet still open as lost, including a winner of a batch that failed or is still being retried. A round call only touches a bet that is open and was never settled (settlement_version 0): a bet that was settled and then reversed back to open is left open (counted in left_open) for you to settle on its own at the next version. The winners are all checked before any money moves: a winner that is not a bet of this round answers 422 wager_not_in_round, and one already settled otherwise than won at version 1 by the game with the same payout and bonus answers 409 settlement_conflict; both list the offending references and settle nothing, so neither is terminal for a round: report those references, remove them from winners and send the call again; the rest of the round is not settled until you do. A winner that was open when checked but is no longer open when its turn comes (a GMS admin void, or your own per-wager settle, in between) is not paid: the call still answers 200 and lists it in skipped_winners, which you should report. Not covered by the idempotency header: the call is safe to repeat. Bets already settled are counted, not touched, and a winner settled by an earlier call with the same amounts counts as already_settled, so a call that timed out or failed can simply be sent again and finishes whatever is still open. A round nobody bet on is created and closed (all counts 0). Allowed while the product is suspended, and never refused for the house's or a player's balance.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
productstringRequiredA product code of the key's game
winnersarrayRequiredThe winning bets, possibly empty, at most 1,000, each reference once
winners[].referencestringRequiredA bet of this round (up to 100 characters)
winners[].payoutnumberRequired0 to 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals: the gross amount credited, as for a single settlement
winners[].bonusnumberOptional0 to 99,999,999.99, up to 2 decimals. payout + bonus may not exceed the product's max_payout
finalbooleanOptionalDefault true: also settle every other open bet as lost and mark the round settled. false settles only the listed winners and closes the round to new bets

Response (200)

{"success": true, "data": {"round": {"reference": "round-1", "product": "crash_classic", "status": "settled", "closed_at": "2026-10-30T10:16:00+00:00"}, "counts": {"won": 2, "lost": 37, "voided": 0, "already_settled": 0, "left_open": 0}, "skipped_winners": []}}

won, lost and voided are the bets this call settled; already_settled the bets of the round that were no longer open (including winners an earlier call settled); left_open the reversed bets that stay open. skipped_winners (always present, empty when none) lists the winners this call did not pay because they were no longer open when locked; they are not in any count.

Errors

Every error is {"success": false, "message": "…", "code": "…", "retryable": false}; retryable is true only for 429 and 5xx.

StatuscodeWhen
403product_not_allowedThe product is unknown, belongs to another game, or is not house-banked (kadi's rake products never take wagers)
409round_closedThe round was voided. A final: false batch can also end with this when the round is voided while the batch runs: the void refunds the bets still open, any winners this batch had already settled stay won, and the batch stops paying
409settlement_conflictA winner was already settled otherwise (lost, void, by an admin, with other amounts, or reversed and reopened); references lists them. Nothing is settled: remove them from winners and send again
422wager_not_in_roundA winner is not a bet of this product and round; references lists them. Nothing is settled: remove them from winners and send again
422payout_above_limitA winner's payout + bonus is above the product's max_payout; references lists them
422validation_failedThe body is invalid (more than 1,000 winners, a repeated reference, …), or reference is longer than 100 characters
429rate_limitedThe 1200/min limit is spent (Retry-After header), retryable true
500server_errorUnexpected failure, retryable true; send the same call again to finish it

Also returns 401 (API key), 403 (route_not_allowed): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/rounds/{reference}/voidVoid a round and refund every open bet (game server)▶

Description

Bets already settled, including winners of an earlier final: false settle, are counted as already_settled and kept; a bet that was reversed back to open is left open (left_open). A settled round cannot be voided. Not covered by the idempotency header: the call is safe to repeat and finishes whatever is still open. A round nobody bet on is created and voided.

PUT/api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/deposit-limits/{period}Set, cut, raise or remove a deposit limit (kadi)▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min

Description

{period} is day, week or month: a rolling 24 hours, 7 days or 30 days. Each limit caps what the player may deposit within its window: paybill and STK top-ups and bet-shop loads, net of reversals, plus STK prompts from the last few minutes whose money has not arrived. A first limit or a lower one applies at once. A higher limit, or removing one, waits 24 hours (until pending_from). The limits cover every game.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
amountinteger|nullRequiredWhole shillings, 10 to 1,000,000; null removes the limit (after 24 hours)

Response (200)

{"success": true, "message": "Deposit limit saved", "data": {"period": "day", "amount": 1000, "pending_amount": 5000, "pending_from": "2026-10-31T11:00:00+03:00"}}

An unknown period answers 404. Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 404 (customer not found), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/exclusionStart a break from play (kadi)▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min

Description

The player asks for a break on kadi. From then until ends_at the player cannot deposit, stake or launch a game (403 player_excluded, with excluded_until), but can log in and withdraw. It covers every game. Their game sessions end at once. A break can be extended but never shortened or ended early, by anyone: there is no route for it. Asking for a cool-off while a self-exclusion runs keeps it a self-exclusion. A break follows the person: it keeps the phone and national ID it was taken with, and every account with that phone or ID (the player's other accounts, a new sign-up, or an account changed onto that phone or ID later) is put on the same break.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
typestringRequiredcool_off or self_exclusion
durationstringRequiredCool-off: 24h, 7d, 30d. Self-exclusion: 6m, 1y, 5y.

Response (201)

{"success": true, "message": "Break started", "data": {"type": "cool_off", "duration": "7d", "starts_at": "2026-10-30T11:00:00+03:00", "ends_at": "2026-11-06T11:00:00+03:00"}}

Response (422)

{"success": false, "message": "The current break already lasts longer. A break can be made longer, but not shorter.", "code": "exclusion_shorter", "excluded_until": "2026-11-29T11:00:00+03:00"}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 404 (customer not found), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

Newest first. Full keys only. Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 404 (customer not found): see Error Responses.

GET/api/v1/responsible-gambling/flagsDeposits credited against a player's own rules (GMS)▶
API Key

Description

Paybill money that has already arrived is always credited, even when the player is inactive, on a break or over their deposit limit (an STK top-up or a shop load would have been refused). Each such deposit is flagged here for review: rg_deposit_inactive, rg_deposit_while_excluded or rg_deposit_over_limit. Unmatched-deposit assigns are flagged the same way. Newest first.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
customerintegerOptionalA customer id
typestringOptionalOne of the three flag types
resolvedbooleanOptional0 open only, 1 resolved only
page / per_pageintegerOptionalper_page 1 to 200, default 50

Response (200)

{"success": true, "data": {"items": [{"id": 412, "type": "rg_deposit_over_limit", "customer_id": 2041, "details": {"deposit_id": 9921, "trans_id": "TJU8XK2LQ1", "amount": 1500, "remaining": 500, "excluded_until": null, "status": 1}, "created_at": "2026-10-30T11:00:00+03:00", "resolved_at": null}], "pagination": {"page": 1, "per_page": 50, "total": 1, "last_page": 1}}}

Full keys only. Also returns 401 (API key), 422 (validation): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/responsible-gambling/flags/{id}/resolveMark a flag reviewed (GMS)▶

Records who reviewed the flag and what they did (for example a refund made by hand). It moves no money. Resolving a resolved flag changes nothing.

Full keys only. An unknown id answers 404. Also returns 401 (API key), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

GET /api/v1/customers List active customers ▶
API Key
200 OK

Active customers (status 1) who signed up on or after 2026-03-01, without the house account. Not paginated. Customer fields: wallet_id and balance appear when the customer has a wallet; deposits and withdraws are the sums of their M-Pesa wallet transactions; shop_loads is cash loaded at bet shops (completed loads, after excise duty, reversed loads left out) and shop_withdrawals is cash paid out at bet shops (paid only, not pending); purchases_* are sums of load, gift and emoji purchases; single_played and competition_played count games and competition entries; coin_wallet_id and coins appear when they have a coin wallet. Money stored on a record (balance) is a string with 2 decimals; computed sums are numbers.

POST /api/v1/customers Create a customer and their wallet ▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min
201 Created 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error

Creates the customer and a wallet with balance 0. A referral_code that is another customer's referral code makes this customer their referral; other codes (agent codes) are stored as they are. A usable promo_code qualifies the customer for the signup bonus; an unknown, expired, deactivated or used-up code is ignored and signup still succeeds.

referral_codestringOptionalReferral or agent code the customer signed up with
promo_codestringOptionalPromo code for the signup bonus, up to 30 characters, any case

PUT /api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier} Update a customer ▶
API KeyEncrypted ID
200 OK 404 Not Found 422 Unprocessable

Send only the fields to change. Uniqueness is not checked here: a value another customer already has fails with 500. A site key may change only name, email and phone_no: status, account_no, google_id and referral_code are refused with 422 ("The status field is prohibited."). Changing status to anything but 1 ends the customer's game sessions (see Launching Games).

google_idstringOptionalGoogle sign-in id
referral_codestringOptionalAgent or referral code
statusintegerOptional1 active, 0 inactive

DELETE /api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier} Delete a customer ▶
API KeyEncrypted ID
200 OK 404 Not Found 409 Conflict

A soft delete: the customer stops appearing in lookups, searches and lists, but the customer row and their wallet are kept, so their history (ledger, deposits, games, finance reports) still points at them. Payments later made to their account number are kept as unmatched deposits, their referral code stops matching signups and they earn no further referral bonuses. Refused with 409 while the customer holds money or has activity in progress; open lists what is in the way: wallet_balance (not 0, including negative), coin_balance, referral_balance, open_game (a stake in a game that still holds money), open_competition, pending_withdrawal (waiting on M-Pesa), pending_referral_withdrawal, pending_hold, open_dispute, pending_shop_withdrawal (a cash withdrawal at a bet shop not yet collected), or house_wallet (the house customer can never be deleted). To stop a customer without deleting them, set status to 0.

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredThe customer: an encrypted customer id or encrypted account number.

Example Request

DELETE /api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier} HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json

Response (200)

{
  "message": "Customer deleted successfully"
}

Response (404)

{
  "message": "Customer not found"
}

Response (409: money or activity still open)

{
  "message": "Customer cannot be deleted while they hold money or have activity in progress",
  "open": [
    "wallet_balance",
    "pending_withdrawal"
  ]
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier): see Error Responses.

PUT /api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/wallet Adjust a customer's wallet by an amount ▶
API KeyEncrypted ID
201 Created 404 Not Found 422 Unprocessable

Adds amount to the balance (negative to take away). A negative amount that would leave the balance below 0 is refused with 422; a positive amount is always accepted, so a balance that is already negative can be brought back up. Recorded in the ledger as an adjustment with the reason and the API key; listed at GET /finance/adjustments. To set an exact balance use PUT /wallets/{id}/balance.

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredThe customer: an encrypted customer id or encrypted account number.

reasonstringOptionalWhy, up to 255 characters. Recorded as unspecified when missing

{
  "amount": -50,
  "reason": "Duplicate credit reversed"
}

  "status": "success"
}

}

}

    ]
  }

200 OK 404 Not Found

Call once the client has verified the customer's email and phone, for every customer. It records verified_at, pays any referral bonus now due and grants the signup bonus when the customer qualifies. Safe to call more than once: nothing is paid twice. Replaces POST /customers/{id}/referral/verified, which does the same and still works.

Also returns 401 (API key): see Error Responses.

Signup bonus. A player who signs up with a valid promo code (promo_code on POST /customers) and is then reported verified (POST /customers/{id}/verified) gets KES 20 in their main wallet. Both signup and verification must happen before the code expires. The house wallet pays it: with excise duty on, the credit is grossed up to KES 21.05, KES 1.05 is excise owed to KRA and the player keeps KES 20. One bonus per phone number.

Locked until played. The KES 20 can be staked on games, tournaments and jackpots, but it cannot be withdrawn, moved to another wallet or spent on coins until the player has staked that much since the bonus (stakes that were refunded do not count). Money the player deposits is never locked. A withdrawal, wallet transfer or coin purchase that would use locked bonus returns 400 with {"code": "signup_bonus_locked", "locked_amount": 20, "available": 5}.

The promotion is switched on, and optionally capped, on the server (PROMOTIONS_SIGNUP_BONUS_ENABLED, PROMOTIONS_SIGNUP_BONUS_BUDGET_CAP). While it is off, codes are not applied at signup and no bonus is paid. Costs are reported at GET /finance/promotions.

POST /api/v1/wallets/transfer/{encryptedIdentifier} Transfer between two wallets ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min
200 OK 400 Bad Request 404 Not Found 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error

Moves amount from the wallet in the path to wallet_id, as a ledgered transfer. The sender's locked signup bonus cannot be moved. Validation failures (for example an amount of 0 or less, or with more than 2 decimals) return 422 with {message, errors}.

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredEncrypted id of the sending wallet

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
amountnumberRequiredKES to move, greater than 0 with at most 2 decimals (otherwise 422)
wallet_idintegerRequiredPlain id of the receiving wallet

Example Request

POST /api/v1/wallets/transfer/{encryptedIdentifier} HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Idempotency-Key: 7c9e6f1a-2b3d-4c5e-8f90-a1b2c3d4e5f6
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "amount": 200,
  "wallet_id": 77
}

Response (200)

{
  "status": "success"
}

Response (400: no receiving wallet given)

{
  "status": "error",
  "message": "Receiver wallet is required"
}

Response (400: not enough balance)

{
  "status": "error",
  "message": "Insufficient balance"
}

Response (400: would move locked bonus)

{
  "status": "error",
  "message": "KES 20 of the signup bonus must be staked before it can be withdrawn or moved",
  "code": "signup_bonus_locked",
  "locked_amount": 20,
  "available": 5
}

Response (404: a wallet does not exist)

{
  "status": "error",
  "message": "Receiver wallet not found"
}

Response (500: validation failed or unexpected error)

{
  "status": "error"
}

Since responsible gambling (P4): the sending player must be active and not on a break: otherwise 403 with code player_inactive / player_excluded. See Responsible Gambling.

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

GET /api/v1/wallets/transactions List wallet-to-wallet transactions ▶
API Key
200 OK 422 Unprocessable

Every wallet transaction: customer transfers and competition house cuts. sender_id and receiver_id are wallet ids (for house cuts the sender is the competition wallet and the receiver the house wallet). Without per_page every row is returned, oldest first; with it, one page, newest first, with links and meta (current_page, last_page, per_page, total) added beside data.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
per_pageintegerOptional1 to 200. Turns paging on
pageintegerOptionalDefault 1

Example Request

GET /api/v1/wallets/transactions HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json

Response (200)

{
  "data": [
    {
      "id": 5120,
      "transaction_id": "TXN20260924081523-6EE9VY",
      "sender_id": 42,
      "receiver_id": 77,
      "initiator_id": 1,
      "amount": "200.00",
      "status": 2
    }
  ]
}

Also returns 401 (API key): see Error Responses.

GET /api/v1/wallets/transactions/{encryptedIdentifier} Get one wallet transaction ▶
API KeyEncrypted ID
200 OK 404 Not Found

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredEncrypted wallet transaction id

Accept: application/json

{
  "data": {
    "id": 5120,
    "transaction_id": "TXN20260924081523-6EE9VY",
    "sender_id": 42,
    "receiver_id": 77,

    "status": 2
  }

Response (404)

{
  "message": "Wallet Transaction not found"
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier): see Error Responses.

Deposits are M-Pesa payments into the paybill. Safaricom confirms each one to KadiApi, which credits the customer the bill ref (the part before any #) names, first match wins: their account number (typing slips in KK- numbers are corrected), their 8-digit player number (spaces and dashes allowed; not when it is another customer's ID number), or, when the bill ref is only a phone number, their phone number in any Kenyan format (0712…, 712…, +254712…, 254712…). A phone is only used when exactly one customer has it and it is verified; it is credited whoever pays. Each deposit records how it matched in matched_by: account_no, player_no, phone (paid from that phone) or phone_other_payer (someone else's number paid). National ID numbers are not matched: an 8-digit ID that is also a player number is left unmatched. A bill ref with letters (SHOP-0712345678, Mary 0712345678) is never read as a phone. For a plain wallet deposit, 5% excise duty is taken when excise duty is on, so the wallet keeps 95% (excise_amount, net_amount); see Excise Duty. A payment whose bill ref matches no customer is kept as an unmatched deposit (status 0, with unmatched_reason unknown, phone_ambiguous (more than one customer has that phone) or phone_unverified) for finance to assign to a customer, refund, or record as house funding (owner money for the house wallet). A payment naming the house account (by account number, player number or phone) is also kept unmatched for that, with no matched_by. Deposit status: 0 unmatched, 1 pending, 2 completed, 4 refunded. msisdn is the payer's phone number as M-Pesa sends it (usually a SHA-256 hash).
GET /api/v1/deposits List all deposits ▶
API Key
200 OK

Every deposit with its excise duty. customer is the credited customer's name when there is one. matched_by says how the bill ref matched (null for unmatched deposits and ones recorded before matching was tracked) and unmatched_reason why it did not. Pass matched_by (account_no, player_no, phone or phone_other_payer) to list only those, e.g. ?matched_by=phone_other_payer to review deposits paid by someone else's number. Not paginated; for ranges, filters and paging use GET /finance/deposits.

Example Request

GET /api/v1/deposits HTTP/1.1

Accept: application/json

Response (200)

{

    {
      "id": 118,

      "trans_type": "Pay Bill",
      "trans_time": "2026-09-24 12:52:08",

      "bill_ref_no": "KK-8FAV7WJY",
      "msisdn": "0daf17cd2c16b58b16f88647305c865debd589bb249142636847c79bbee14922",
      "name": "JANE W WANJIRU",
      "status": 2,
      "matched_by": "account_no",
      "unmatched_reason": null,
      "excise_amount": "5.00",

POST /api/v1/deposits/{encryptedIdentifier} Send an STK Push deposit prompt to the customer's phone ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent5/min
201 Created 404 Not Found 500 Server Error

Asks M-Pesa to prompt the customer's phone for amount (whole shillings; decimals are dropped). 201 only means the prompt was requested: the wallet is credited when Safaricom confirms the payment, like any paybill deposit. The M-Pesa account reference sent is the customer's player_no (8 digits), so the confirmation is matched back by player number (matched_by: player_no) whatever the player later saves as their ID number. The identifier may be the encrypted customer id, account number or player number. Validation failures and M-Pesa errors return 500.

}

Since responsible gambling (P4): refused before the prompt is sent when the player is inactive or on a break (403 player_inactive / player_excluded) or the amount is above what their deposit limits leave (422 deposit_limit with remaining). See Responsible Gambling.

GET /api/v1/deposits/unmatched Unmatched deposits (the finance work queue) ▶
API Key
200 OK 422 Unprocessable

Deposits whose bill ref matched no account, oldest first, each with suggested customers (strongest match first): account_no (the bill ref is an account number), player_no (the bill ref is a player number), payer_phone (the paying number is a customer's phone) and bill_ref_phone (the bill ref, read as a phone number, is a customer's phone). ambiguous is true when that phone belongs to more than one customer. Phone suggestions ignore verification (an unverified or shared phone is why the deposit was left unmatched; see unmatched_reason). Suggestions are hints; a person decides. msisdn is masked when it is a phone number. Pass status=assigned, refunded, house_funded or shop_float to list resolved ones (newest first, no suggestions). An unmatched deposit whose bill ref names a bet shop (SHOP-<shop_no>) has that shop in bet_shop (id, shop_no, name, status; otherwise null): it is the owner buying float, to credit with POST /deposits/{id}/shop-float. summary always counts every deposit still unmatched, whatever the filters. bill_ref_no is not masked here. unmatched_reason is unknown, phone_ambiguous or phone_unverified (null for deposits recorded before it was tracked). resolution has action, customer_id, customer_name, account_no_used, ledger_entry_id, mpesa_reference, note, resolved_by and resolved_at; for action shop_float it also has float_purchase_id (the one to void if it went to the wrong shop), bet_shop_id, shop_no and shop_name.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
statusstringOptionalunmatched (default), assigned, refunded, house_funded or shop_float
fromstringOptionalY-m-d, received on or after
tostringOptionalY-m-d, on or before; not before from
per_pageintegerOptional1 to 200, default 50
pageintegerOptionalDefault 1

Example Request

GET /api/v1/deposits/unmatched?per_page=20 HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "summary": {

      "unmatched_amount": 4500.0
    },
    "items": [
      {
        "id": 131,
        "trans_id": "UJ2KQ8B4ZT",
        "trans_time": "2026-09-24 14:10:00",
        "amount": 250.0,
        "bill_ref_no": "0712345678",
        "msisdn": "2547****5678",
        "name": "JANE W WANJIRU",
        "status": 0,

        "suggestions": [
          {
            "customer_id": 42,
            "name": "Jane Wanjiru",
            "account_no": "KK-8FAV7WJY",
            "player_no": "12345671",
            "phone_no": "254712345678",
            "match": "bill_ref_phone",
            "matches": [
              "bill_ref_phone"
            ],
            "ambiguous": false

        ],

      }
    ],

POST /api/v1/deposits/unmatched/match Assign unmatched deposits whose bill ref now matches a customer ▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min
200 OK

For payers who paid before registering, before their phone was verified, or whose account number was corrected. Uses the same rule as a live paybill deposit: account number, player number, or a phone held by exactly one customer and verified (see Deposits above). Never a national ID number, never the house account (record that as house funding), and never a phone match paid from someone else's number (phone_other_payer): that one stays unmatched with its suggestion for a person to assign. Assigned deposits get matched_by. Each item has matched_by, and the resolution note reads Matched by <matched_by> <bill ref>. With dry_run (the default) nothing changes and items lists what would be assigned. Each assignment is credited like a deposit (excise duty, referral bonus) and records the calling API key in resolved_by. The same matcher runs as php artisan deposits:match-unmatched.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
dry_runbooleanOptionalDefault true

Example Request

POST /api/v1/deposits/unmatched/match HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "dry_run": false
}

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "dry_run": false,
    "matched": 2,
    "matched_amount": 350.0,
    "assigned": 2,
    "assigned_amount": 350.0,
    "items": [
      {
        "deposit_id": 131,
        "trans_id": "UJ2KQ8B4ZT",
        "amount": 250.0,
        "bill_ref_no": "kk-8fav7wjy",
        "customer_id": 42,
        "account_no": "KK-8FAV7WJY",
        "player_no": "12345671",
        "matched_by": "account_no",
        "assigned": true,
        "message": "Deposit credited to the customer"
      }
    ]
  }
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

POST /api/v1/deposits/{encryptedIdentifier}/house-funding Credit an unmatched deposit to the house wallet (owner funding) ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min
200 OK 404 Not Found 409 Conflict 422 Unprocessable

How the owner tops up the house wallet, which pays signup bonuses: pay the paybill with bill ref HOUSE (anything that matches no account works). The payment arrives as an unmatched deposit; record it here. The full amount is credited to the house wallet as house_funding (ledger category capital): no excise duty, no referral bonus, and it is not counted as a customer deposit. Cash flow shows it as cash_in.house_funding. A payment made with the house customer's own account number is also left unmatched for this, never credited as a deposit, and it cannot be assigned to the house customer (422, code: house_customer). Undo a mistake with POST /finance/house-funding/{id}/void.

encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredEncrypted deposit id

Request Body

notestringRequiredWhat the money is for, 3 to 255 characters

POST /api/v1/withdraw/{encryptedIdentifier} Pay a customer from their wallet to M-Pesa (B2C) ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent5/min
201 Created 400 Bad Request 404 Not Found 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error

Takes amount off the wallet at once, then sends it to the customer's phone_no by M-Pesa B2C. If M-Pesa refuses the request the debit is reversed straight away (500). 201 means M-Pesa accepted the request; the final result arrives later from Safaricom, and a failed payout is reversed then. Locked signup bonus cannot be withdrawn. Send an Idempotency-Key: without one, a second withdrawal of the same amount the same day is not paid (see Idempotency).

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredThe customer: an encrypted customer id or encrypted account number.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
amountnumberRequiredKES, at least 1

Example Request

POST /api/v1/withdraw/{encryptedIdentifier} HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Idempotency-Key: 9a8b7c6d-5e4f-4a3b-8c2d-1e0f9a8b7c6d
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "amount": 500
}

Response (201: sent to M-Pesa)

{
  "status": "success",
  "ledger_entry_id": "4e1f2a3b-5c6d-4e7f-8091-a2b3c4d5e6f7"
}

Response (400: not enough balance)

{
  "status": "Insufficient wallet balance for withdrawal",
  "ledger_entry_id": null
}

Response (400: no phone number)

{
  "status": "Customer phone number not found",
  "ledger_entry_id": null
}

Response (400: would take locked bonus)

{
  "status": "KES 20 of the signup bonus must be staked before it can be withdrawn or moved",
  "ledger_entry_id": null,
  "code": "signup_bonus_locked",
  "locked_amount": 20,
  "available": 5
}

Response (404: no customer, or no wallet)

{
  "status": "Customer not found",
  "ledger_entry_id": null
}

Response (422: validation failed)

{
  "status": {
    "amount": [
      "The amount field must be at least 1."
    ]
  }
}

Response (500: M-Pesa refused; the debit was reversed)

{
  "status": "Unknown Error",
  "ledger_entry_id": null
}

The outcome is in status: "success", or the error message. Check code for the locked-bonus case.

Since responsible gambling (P4): amount must be whole shillings from 50 to 150,000 (422). A suspended player (status 2, 5 or an undefined code) gets 403 player_suspended; a phone kadi has not verified gets 422 phone_unverified; more than 5 withdrawals an hour (main and referral wallet together) get 429 too_many_withdrawals with retry_after in seconds. A player on a break may still withdraw. See Responsible Gambling. Withdrawals of KES 20,000 or more are held for approval (P4b): the money leaves the wallet, M-Pesa is not called yet, and the answer is 202 {"status": "awaiting_approval", "withdrawal_id": 812, "ledger_entry_id": "…"}. GMS approves (pays the phone verified at this moment) or rejects (refunds); the player may cancel it on kadi.

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

GET/api/v1/withdrawals/pendingWithdrawals waiting for approval (GMS)▶
API Key

Description

M-Pesa withdrawals of KES 20,000 or more held for a decision, oldest first. The money is already out of the player's wallet. Full keys only.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
customerintegerOptionalA customer id
page / per_pageintegerOptionalper_page 1 to 200, default 50

Response (200)

{"success": true, "data": {"items": [{"withdrawal_id": 812, "customer_id": 2041, "player_no": "48213907", "name": "Jane Wanjiru", "amount": 25000, "phone_no": "254712345678", "status": "awaiting", "requested_by": "api_key:5", "requested_at": "2026-10-30T12:00:00+03:00", "admin_user": null, "decided_by": null, "reason": null, "decided_at": null, "payout_status": "pending", "payout_error": null}], "pagination": {"page": 1, "per_page": 50, "total": 1, "last_page": 1}}}

payout_status says where the money is: pending (waiting for a decision), sent (M-Pesa accepted it), failed (M-Pesa refused, or rejected: refunded), cancelled (refunded), or unconfirmed (approved but M-Pesa never answered). payout_error is the reason when there is one.

Reconciliation warns (withdrawals_awaiting_approval) on any waiting over 24 hours. Also returns 401 (API key), 422 (validation): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/withdrawals/{id}/approveApprove a held withdrawal and pay it (GMS)▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min

Description

{id} is the withdrawal_id. Records the decision, then asks M-Pesa to pay the phone verified when the withdrawal was asked for (not the player's current number). A withdrawal is decided once: the approval stands even when M-Pesa refuses the payout, in which case the player is refunded at once (502). The B2C result then settles it like any other withdrawal. Full keys only.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
admin_userstringRequiredThe GMS user approving it (up to 100 characters)

Response (200 / 502)

{"success": true, "message": "Approved: the payout was sent to M-Pesa", "data": {"withdrawal_id": 812, "status": "approved", "admin_user": "ann", "decided_by": "api_key:3", "…": "…"}}
{"success": false, "message": "Approved, but M-Pesa refused the payout; the player has been refunded", "data": {"withdrawal_id": 812, "status": "approved", "…": "…"}}

Response (504)

{"success": false, "code": "payout_unknown", "message": "Approved, but M-Pesa did not answer: the payout may or may not have been made. Do not retry; check the M-Pesa statement.", "data": {"withdrawal_id": 812, "status": "approved", "payout_status": "unconfirmed", "…": "…"}}

M-Pesa timed out or could not be reached. The approval stands and nothing is refunded, because M-Pesa may have paid. Check the M-Pesa statement before doing anything else; the withdrawal also shows in reconciliation as stuck.

Response (404 / 409)

{"success": false, "message": "No withdrawal waiting for approval has that id", "code": "not_found"}
{"success": false, "message": "This withdrawal has already been decided", "code": "not_awaiting", "status": "rejected"}

Also returns 401 (API key), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

404 and 409 as for approve. Also returns 401 (API key), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 422 (validation), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

POST/api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/withdrawals/{id}/cancelThe player cancels a held withdrawal (kadi)▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min

Description

A player takes back their own withdrawal of KES 20,000 or more while it waits for approval. {id} is the withdrawal_id from the 202. The money returns to the wallet at once; kadi's balance updates through the wallet webhook. Another player's withdrawal is not found (404); a decided one gets 409 not_awaiting. A held withdrawal never expires by itself: it waits until GMS decides it or the player cancels it.

Response (200)

{"success": true, "message": "Cancelled: the money is back in the wallet", "data": {"withdrawal_id": 812, "amount": 25000, "status": "cancelled", "reason": "Cancelled by the player", "…": "…"}}

Games and products. A game is one app on the platform (kadi, sportsbook, crash, slots and scratch cards): it has a lobby tile and the launch_url kadi sends a player to. A product is a revenue line finance reports on, and belongs to one game: kadi has kadi (single games), kadi_tournament and kadi_jackpot. revenue_model is rake (the house takes a cut of player-vs-player pots) or house_banked (players play against the house). Every ledger entry of a game carries its product; deposits, withdrawals, excise duty, promotions, referrals, bet shop float and adjustments carry none (platform). Codes never change. New games and products are added on the server (php artisan game:create, php artisan product:create). status for both: active, suspended or retired.
GET/api/v1/gamesThe lobby: games and their products▶
API Key

Description

Active games by default, in lobby order (sort_order), each with its products. Not paginated.

statusstringOptionalactive (default), suspended, retired or all

Response (200)

    {
      "code": "kadi", "name": "Kadi", "launch_url": "https://kadikings.co.ke/play", "status": "active", "sort_order": 1,
      "products": [
        {"code": "kadi", "game": "kadi", "name": "Kadi", "revenue_model": "rake", "status": "active", "min_stake": null, "max_stake": null, "max_payout": null, "winnings_tax_rate": 0},
        {"code": "kadi_tournament", "game": "kadi", "name": "Kadi Tournament", "revenue_model": "rake", "status": "active", "min_stake": null, "max_stake": null, "max_payout": null, "winnings_tax_rate": 0},
        {"code": "kadi_jackpot", "game": "kadi", "name": "Kadi Jackpot", "revenue_model": "rake", "status": "active", "min_stake": null, "max_stake": null, "max_payout": null, "winnings_tax_rate": 0}
      ]
    }
  ]
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 422 (unknown status): see Error Responses.
