Kadi is the card game players play against each other. The house does not bet: it takes a cut of each pot (a rake). Single games, tournaments and jackpots each have their own escrow wallets. Every endpoint below is used only by kadi; each links to its full documentation on the Kadi pages.

Revenue modelrake: the house takes a share of player-vs-player pots (rates in the fee schedule)
Productskadi (single games), kadi_tournament, kadi_jackpot
KeyThe kadi game server uses a full key; the kadi site uses its site key for complaints
LaunchKadi's own play form, not the launch-token flow of the other games

The flow

new game     ── POST /game/wallets ──────────▶ the game's escrow wallet
each player  ── POST /game/bets ─────────────▶ stake into the game wallet
game ends    ── POST /game/withdraw/{id} ────▶ winner paid (less 5%)
             └─ /game/drop/{id} | /game/credit | /game/refund for dropped or cancelled games

Single games

  1. Open a game wallet
    The escrow for one game. Status 1 open, 3 closed.
  2. Each player stakes
    The stake leaves the player's wallet and goes into the game wallet.
  3. Pay the winner
    Closes the game; a further 5% is taken, rounded down.
  4. Dropped or cancelled games
    Settle a game where players disconnected, pay each remaining player their share of a voided game, or refund every stake.
  5. Results and income
    Decided games with each player's result, and the house share by number of players.

Tournaments and jackpots

  1. Open the player's competition wallet
    One wallet per player per competition (cmp_uid).
  2. Enter
    The entry fee moves from the wallet into the competition wallet, less the house share (10% tournament, 20% jackpot).
  3. After each round
    The loser's whole competition balance moves to the winner.
  4. Pay out
    The competition wallet is paid to the customer's main wallet and closed.
  5. Results and income
    Round results, winners, and the house share by number of rounds.

Disputed results

  1. A player complains (kadi site)
    The disputed winnings are held in dispute escrow at once.
  2. GMS decides
    Valid: the disputed transactions are reversed. Invalid or withdrawn: the held money goes back.

Admin corrections (overwriting or deleting a game wallet, game transaction, competition wallet or competition transaction) are on the Kadi pages: Game Wallets, Game Bets, Competition Wallets, Competition Entries.

Players bet against the house on sports events. One bet slip is one wager: its stake leaves the player's wallet when the slip is placed and is settled when every selection is decided, through your outbox, so a settlement is never lost.

Revenue modelhouse_banked: the house is the other side of every bet
Productssportsbook (bet slips), sportsbook_jackpot (jackpot entries)
KeyA game key bound to this game (X-API-KEY), plus the player token (Authorization: Bearer) on player routes
Rate limit1,200 wager calls a minute per key, raised with the key multiplier (up to 20×); 429 is retryable
SettlementAsynchronous and versioned: one outbox row per settlement, delivered in order per slip

The flow

kadi         ── launch token ──▶ sportsbook ── POST /launch-tokens/redeem ──▶ player token
slip placed  ── POST /wagers ──────────────────────▶ stake into the slip's escrow (open)
slip decided ── POST /wagers/{ref}/settle  v1 ─────▶ won | lost | void   (from the outbox)
result fixed ── POST /wagers/{ref}/reverse v1 ─────▶ open again ──▶ settle v2 …
timeout      ── GET /wagers/{ref}?product= ────────▶ what happened to the slip

Endpoints this game calls, in order

  1. Start the session
    Once per launch: kadi posts a launch token to your launch_url; trade it for a player token good for 12 hours in your game only. Do not retry on a timeout (the token may already be used): send the player back to kadi to relaunch.
  2. Show the player and their balance
    The player has one wallet across every game. Read the balance again after a bet; wallet_version tells a stale balance from a fresh one.
  3. Place a bet slip
    Synchronous, one call per slip: reference is your slip id; no round_reference. A free bet sends free_bet_amount instead of stake. Jackpot entries use product sportsbook_jackpot. Write the slip locally as pending first; mark it open on 201/200, rejected on any other 4xx except 429.
  4. Settle the slip
    When every selection is decided, from your outbox, versions of one slip in order. payout includes the stake on a cash win; a multibet bonus goes in bonus; a slip on a cancelled event settles void (the stake goes back). Winnings tax is withheld by the API at the product's rate. 409 version_out_of_order: retry later.
  5. Correct a result
    A result changed after you settled: reverse the current version (the slip reopens with its stake back in escrow), then settle the next version with the right result. The player may go below zero; that is allowed.
  6. Find out what happened
    After a timeout on a place or a settle, before doing anything else. A slip that was never placed answers 404: send a void settle for it (a tombstone) and mark it void.

Endpoint details

An Aviator-style game: players bet during a short betting window, then cash out before the plane crashes. Every bet belongs to a round, and the whole round is settled in one call when the plane crashes.

Revenue modelhouse_banked: the house is the other side of every bet
Productscrash_classic (the examples on this page)
KeyA game key bound to this game (X-API-KEY), plus the player token (Authorization: Bearer) on player routes
Rate limit1,200 wager calls a minute per key, raised with the key multiplier (up to 20×); 429 is retryable
SettlementPer round: the winners in one call (batches of up to 1,000), every other bet loses

The flow

kadi           ── launch token ──▶ crash ── POST /launch-tokens/redeem ──▶ player token
betting window ── POST /wagers {round_reference} ──▶ each bet: stake into escrow
manual cash-out ─ POST /wagers/{ref}/settle v1 won ─▶ paid at once (optional)
plane crashes  ── POST /rounds/{ref}/settle {winners} ─▶ winners paid, the rest lost
round broken   ── POST /rounds/{ref}/void ───────────▶ every open bet refunded

Endpoints this game calls, in order

  1. Start the session
    Once per launch: kadi posts a launch token to your launch_url; trade it for a player token good for 12 hours in your game only. Do not retry on a timeout (the token may already be used): send the player back to kadi to relaunch.
  2. Show the player and their balance
    The player has one wallet across every game. Read the balance again after a bet; wallet_version tells a stale balance from a fresh one.
  3. Place a bet
    During the betting window, one call per bet with round_reference = the round id (the round is created on first use). A player may have several bets in a round. Once the round is closed a place answers 409 round_closed.
  4. Pay a manual cash-out at once (optional)
    To show the money before the round ends: settle that bet won at version 1 with its payout. Leave it out of the round's winners, or send it there with the same payout (it then counts as already_settled).
  5. Settle the round
    When the plane crashes: the round's winners with their payouts (auto cash-outs included), at most 1,000 per call. A larger round goes in batches with final: false, then one final: true call, sent only after every batch answered 200. Every other open bet loses. Safe to repeat.
  6. Void a broken round
    The round cannot finish (a server fault before the flight ends): every open bet is refunded and the round is closed. Safe to repeat.
  7. Correct a settled bet
    Rare. Reverse the bet's current version, then settle it on its own at the next version: round calls leave a reopened bet open.
  8. Find out what happened
    After a timeout on a place or a settle, before doing anything else.

Endpoint details

Each spin is decided by your game the moment it is played, so a spin is one call: the stake goes in and any win comes out in one transaction. There is nothing to settle later and no outbox. Refunds are not part of the flow.

Revenue modelhouse_banked: the house is the other side of every spin
ProductsOne per slot title, e.g. slots_fruit_mania, each with its own max_stake and max_payout
KeyA game key bound to this game (X-API-KEY), plus the player token (Authorization: Bearer) on player routes
Rate limit1,200 wager calls a minute per key, raised with the key multiplier (up to 20×); 429 is retryable. One spin is one call: size the multiplier for peak spins a minute
SettlementInstant: placed and settled in the same call

The flow

kadi    ── launch token ──▶ slots ── POST /launch-tokens/redeem ──▶ player token
spin    ── POST /wagers/instant {stake, outcome, payout} ──▶ stake in, win out, one transaction
timeout ── the same call again (a replay) ──▶ then GET /wagers/{ref}?product=
fault   ── POST /wagers/{ref}/reverse v1 ──▶ POST /wagers/{ref}/settle v2   (rare)

Endpoints this game calls, in order

  1. Start the session
    Once per launch: kadi posts a launch token to your launch_url; trade it for a player token good for 12 hours in your game only. Do not retry on a timeout (the token may already be used): send the player back to kadi to relaunch.
  2. Show the player and their balance
    The player has one wallet across every game. Read the balance again after a bet; wallet_version tells a stale balance from a fresh one.
  3. Play a spin
    One call per spin, after your game has decided it: reference = the spin id, outcome won or lost, payout the gross win including the stake (0 for a loss). A free spin sends funding: free_bet and free_bet_amount. A 403 or 422 means the spin did not happen: show it as not played. A 409 reference_conflict does not mean that: look the spin up.
  4. Find out what happened
    After a timeout: first send the same call again (a replay answers 200 and moves no money); if that fails too, look the spin up. 404 means no money moved and the spin did not happen.
  5. Undo a wrong spin (rare)
    A spin was paid wrongly (a machine fault): reverse version 1. The stake goes back into the spin's escrow and the spin is open again.
  6. Settle the corrected spin
    Only after a reverse: settle the right result at version 2 (or ask GMS to void the spin).

Endpoint details

A card bought and revealed at once is one call, like a slot spin. A card bought now and revealed later is a normal bet: placed at purchase, settled when it is revealed. Refunds are not part of the flow.

Revenue modelhouse_banked: the house is the other side of every card
ProductsOne per card type and price, e.g. scratch_gold_100: min_stake = max_stake = the price, max_payout = the top prize
KeyA game key bound to this game (X-API-KEY), plus the player token (Authorization: Bearer) on player routes
Rate limit1,200 wager calls a minute per key, raised with the key multiplier (up to 20×); 429 is retryable
SettlementInstant when revealed at purchase; otherwise placed, then settled at reveal

The flow

kadi                ── launch token ──▶ scratch ── POST /launch-tokens/redeem ──▶ player token
buy + reveal at once ── POST /wagers/instant ──────▶ one transaction
buy now             ── POST /wagers ─────────────▶ price into escrow (open)
reveal later        ── POST /wagers/{ref}/settle v1 ─▶ won | lost

Endpoints this game calls, in order

  1. Start the session
    Once per launch: kadi posts a launch token to your launch_url; trade it for a player token good for 12 hours in your game only. Do not retry on a timeout (the token may already be used): send the player back to kadi to relaunch.
  2. Show the player and their balance
    The player has one wallet across every game. Read the balance again after a bet; wallet_version tells a stale balance from a fresh one.
  3. Buy and reveal a card
    One call per card: stake = the card price (any other amount answers 422 stake_below_limit or stake_above_limit), outcome and payout from the card. A free card sends funding: free_bet and free_bet_amount. A 403 or 422 means the card was not sold; a 409 reference_conflict does not: look the card up.
  4. Buy a card to reveal later
    The price leaves the wallet at purchase and the card stays open until it is revealed. reference = the card id.
  5. Reveal the card
    Settle the card won or lost at version 1 when the player reveals it, through your outbox.
  6. Find out what happened
    After a timeout: send the same call again first (a replay moves no money), then look the card up. 404 means it was not sold.
  7. Correct a card (rare)
    A card was paid wrongly: reverse its current version, then settle the next version with the right result.

Endpoint details

How a single game works. The game server creates a game wallet (an escrow for one game), then each player stakes with POST /game/bets: the stake leaves their wallet and goes into the game wallet. The game ends in one of four ways: POST /game/withdraw/{id} pays the winner (less a further 5%, rounded down), POST /game/drop/{id} settles a game where players disconnected, POST /game/credit pays each remaining player their share when a dropped player voids the game, and POST /game/refund gives every stake back. Game wallet status: 1 open, 3 closed. game_type is set by the game server (default 1). Fees are in the fee schedule.

POST /api/v1/game/credit Pay a remaining player's share of a voided game ▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min
201 Created 404 Not Found 409 Conflict 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error

Used when a player drops out of a 3 or 4 player game and the game is void: the pot is shared between the players still in. The game server works out each share (the pot less the house's cut, split equally) and calls this once per remaining player. The share is paid from the game wallet into the player's wallet and the game is closed (status 3). Later shares of the same game are still accepted; once less than one share is left in the game wallet, that rest is the house's cut and moves to the house wallet. Each player can be paid once per game, only players who staked in the game can be paid, and a share can't be more than the game wallet holds. A game already paid out or refunded is refused.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
customer_idintegerRequiredPlain customer id
game_wallet_idintegerRequiredPlain game wallet id
amountnumberRequiredKES, at least 0.01

Example Request

POST /api/v1/game/credit HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Idempotency-Key: 3b5d7f91-2c4e-4a6b-8d0f-1a3c5e7b9d2f
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "customer_id": 42,
  "game_wallet_id": 881,
  "amount": 25
}

Response (201)

{
  "status": "success"
}

Response (409: already paid, game closed, or share too large)

{
  "status": "error",
  "error": "Customer has already been paid for this game"
}

Other 409 errors: Game Wallet is already closed, Share is more than the game wallet balance. 404: Game Wallet not found, Customer wallet not found. 422: Customer did not stake in this game.

Response (422: validation failed)

{
  "message": "The selected game wallet id is invalid.",
  "errors": {
    "game_wallet_id": [
      "The selected game wallet id is invalid."
    ]
  }
}

Response (500)

{
  "status": "error",
  "error": "An error occurred while crediting the share."
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

POST /api/v1/game/withdraw/{encryptedIdentifier} Pay a game's winner and close the game ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min
201 Created 400 Bad Request 404 Not Found 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error 409 Conflict

Pays the game wallet's whole balance to customer_id (the winner), less a 5% house share rounded down to a whole shilling (so it never takes more than 5%), then closes the game (status 3). Only an open game can be paid. A game that is already settled (paid out, dropped or refunded) is refused with 409 {"code": "game_closed"} and nothing moves, so a repeated or late call never pays twice. The winner must have staked in this game (a deposit on POST /game/bets); anyone else is refused with 422 {"code": "not_a_player"} and nothing moves.

POST /api/v1/game/drop/{encryptedIdentifier} Settle a game after players disconnected ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min
201 Created 400 Bad Request 404 Not Found 500 Server Error 409 Conflict

game: 1 (the game had started): the dropped players' stakes, less a 10% house share (rounded up), are split equally between the active players (rounded down). game: 0 (not started): each dropped player gets their stake back. The game is then closed. Ids are plain customer ids and every active/dropped id must be in players. Only an open game can be dropped. A game that is already settled (paid out, dropped or refunded) is refused with 409 {"code": "game_closed"} and nothing moves, so a repeated or late call never pays twice.

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredEncrypted game wallet id or encrypted game_id

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
playersarrayRequiredEvery player's customer id
activearrayRequiredPlayers still connected (needed when game is 1)
droppedarrayRequiredPlayers who dropped, at least one
gameintegerOptional1 if the game had started, otherwise 0 (default)

Example Request

}

POST /api/v1/game/bets Stake into a game ▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min
201 Created 400 Bad Request 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error 409 Conflict

The stake endpoint: debits the customer's wallet and credits the full amount to the game wallet, whatever payment_type says. The house takes its share when the game is settled. The player's wallet and the game wallet are locked while the stake is taken, so two stakes at once cannot spend the same money. A settled game takes no more stakes: 409 {"code": "game_closed"}.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
game_wallet_idintegerRequiredPlain game wallet id
customer_idintegerRequiredPlain customer id of an existing customer
payment_typestringRequiredStored as given, e.g. deposit
amountnumberRequiredKES, greater than 0 with at most 2 decimals (otherwise 422)
statusintegerOptionalStored as given
bet_shop_nostringOptionalThe bet shop the stake was made at, when the player is at a shop desktop (any case). Must be an active shop, otherwise 422 (No bet shop has that number. or The bet shop is not active.) and nothing is staked. Stored as bet_shop_id on this stake only, so one game can mix shops and online players; payouts and refunds are never attributed. Leave it out online.

Example Request

POST /api/v1/game/bets HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "game_wallet_id": 881,
  "customer_id": 42,
  "payment_type": "deposit",
  "amount": 100,
  "status": 1
}

Response (201)

{
  "status": "success"
}

Response (400: no game, customer or balance)

{
  "error": "Insufficient balance or Wallet not found."
}

Response (422: validation failed)

{
  "message": "The amount field is required.",
  "errors": {
    "amount": [
      "The amount field is required."
    ]
  }
}

Response (500)

{
  "error": "..."
}

Since responsible gambling (P4): only an active player who is not on a break may stake: otherwise 403 {"error": "…", "code": "player_inactive" | "player_excluded"} (with excluded_until for a break), and nothing is debited. See Responsible Gambling.

Also returns 401 (API key), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

  "message": "Game Transaction not found"
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier): see Error Responses.

How competitions work. A tournament (game_type 1) or jackpot (2) is identified by cmp_uid. Each player has their own competition wallet in it. Entering (POST /competition/transactions) moves the entry fee from their wallet into the competition wallet, less the house share (10% tournament, 20% jackpot), and raises their level by 1. After each round, POST /competition/payout moves the loser's whole competition balance to the winner (level down 1 for the loser, up 1 for the winner). POST /competition/withdraw/{id} pays a competition wallet out to a customer's main wallet and closes it. No house share is taken at payout or withdrawal. Competition wallet status: 1 open, 3 closed. A wallet with a pending complaint is frozen.
GET /api/v1/competition/wallets List all competition wallets ▶
API Key
200 OK 422 Unprocessable

Without per_page every row is returned, oldest first; with it, one page, newest first, with links and meta (current_page, last_page, per_page, total) added beside data. For date ranges and filters use GET /finance/competitions.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
per_pageintegerOptional1 to 200. Turns paging on
pageintegerOptionalDefault 1

Example Request

  ]

POST /api/v1/competition/payout Move a round's result: loser's balance to the winner ▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min
200 OK 400 Bad Request 404 Not Found 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error

Moves the sender's (loser's) whole competition balance to the receiver (winner), records a loss and a win, and moves the levels. Both wallets must be open, in the same competition and game type, not frozen by a complaint, and the sender must have a balance. With receiver_withdraw: true the receiver's wallet is then paid out to its owner (as /competition/withdraw); if that fails the transfer still stands and withdrawal says why.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
sender_competition_wallet_idintegerRequiredPlain id of the losing wallet
receiver_competition_wallet_idintegerRequiredPlain id of the winning wallet
receiver_withdrawbooleanOptionalAlso pay the receiver out. Default false

Example Request

POST /api/v1/competition/payout HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "sender_competition_wallet_id": 511,
  "receiver_competition_wallet_id": 510,
  "receiver_withdraw": false
}

Response (200)

{
  "status": "success"
}

Response (200: with receiver_withdraw)

{
  "status": "success",
  "withdrawal": {
    "status": "success"
  }
}

Response (400: a rule was not met)

{
  "error": "Competition Wallet is frozen by a pending complaint"
}

Response (404: a wallet does not exist)

{
  "error": "Invalid Competition Wallet(s)"
}

Response (422: validation failed)

{
  "message": "The sender competition wallet id field is required.",
  "errors": {
    "sender_competition_wallet_id": [
      "The sender competition wallet id field is required."
    ]
  }
}

Response (500)

{
  "error": "An error occurred during the competition wallet payout process."
}

Other 400 messages: Sender and Receiver Competition Wallets must be different, Sender Competition Wallet is not open, Receiver Competition Wallet is not open, Insufficient balance in Sender Competition Wallet, Competition Wallets must have same game type, Competition Wallets must have same competition.

Also returns 401 (API key), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

POST /api/v1/competition/transactions Enter a competition (pay the entry fee) ▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min
201 Created 400 Bad Request 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error

Debits amount from the customer's wallet into their competition wallet, less the house share (10% tournament, 20% jackpot, rounded to the cent), and raises the wallet's level by 1. The customer must own the competition wallet.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
competition_wallet_idintegerRequiredPlain competition wallet id
customer_idintegerRequiredPlain customer id: the wallet's owner
payment_typestringRequiredStored as given, e.g. deposit
amountnumberRequiredEntry fee in KES, greater than 0 with at most 2 decimals (otherwise 422)
bet_shop_nostringOptionalThe bet shop the entry was made at, when the player is at a shop desktop (any case). Must be an active shop, otherwise 422 (No bet shop has that number. or The bet shop is not active.) and nothing is entered. Stored as bet_shop_id on this entry only, so one game can mix shops and online players; payouts and refunds are never attributed. Leave it out online.

Example Request

POST /api/v1/competition/transactions HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "competition_wallet_id": 510,
  "customer_id": 42,
  "payment_type": "deposit",
  "amount": 100
}

Response (201)

{
  "status": "Success"
}

Response (400: not the owner)

{
  "status": "Customer ID do not match"
}

Response (400: not enough balance)

{
  "status": "Insufficient balance or Wallet not found."
}

Response (400: competition wallet closed)

{
  "status": "Competition Wallet is not open"
}

Response (422: validation failed)

{
  "message": "The selected competition wallet id is invalid.",
  "errors": {
    "competition_wallet_id": [
      "The selected competition wallet id is invalid."
    ]
  }
}

Response (500)

{
  "status": "Error creating Competition Transaction"
}

Since responsible gambling (P4): only an active player who is not on a break may stake: otherwise 403 {"status": "…", "code": "player_inactive" | "player_excluded"}. See Responsible Gambling.

Also returns 401 (API key), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

DELETE /api/v1/competition/transactions/{encryptedIdentifier} Delete a competition transaction (admin correction) ▶
API KeyEncrypted ID
200 OK 404 Not Found

Deletes the record only. Money already moved is not reversed.

Path Parameters

Example Request

Response (200)

{
  "status": "Success"
}

Response (404)

{
  "status": "Competition Transaction not found"
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier): see Error Responses.

A player complains about the result of a game, tournament or jackpot. Filing a complaint moves the disputed winnings out of the winner's reach straight away and holds them in dispute escrow until the complaint is closed. A complaint is pending_dispute until it is resolved (valid: the disputed transactions are reversed and the players who paid for them are refunded), rejected (found invalid) or cancelled (withdrawn); the last two give the held money back. Held money is reported as disputed_funds on the balance sheet.
POST/api/v1/complaintsFile a complaint and hold the disputed winnings▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min

Description

Send exactly one of game_wallet_id or competition_wallet_id. What is held depends on the subject:

SubjectDisputed transaction(s)Held from
GameThe winner's payout / payout|dropped game transactions (the house's and the complainant's own are left out)The winner's wallet
Tournament or jackpot, competition wallet open (status 1)The named win or deposit transactions on that wallet, or the latest win when none are namedThe competition wallet itself
Tournament or jackpot, competition wallet closedThe payout transaction(s) of that walletThe winner's wallet

The wallet balance drops by the held amount (ledger entry dispute_hold). When the wallet no longer has enough, whatever is there is held and the rest is recorded as shortfall_amount; a balance never goes negative. While a competition wallet has a pending complaint it is frozen: /competition/payout to or from it and /competition/withdraw return 400. The winner's M-Pesa withdrawals are not blocked; they can only use what is left.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
customer_idintegerRequiredThe complainant. Must have played: a deposit in that game, or a competition wallet in the same competition (cmp_uid). Cannot complain about their own competition wallet
game_wallet_idintegerOne ofThe game complained about
competition_wallet_idintegerOne ofThe winner's competition wallet. Tournament or jackpot follows its game_type
transaction_idsinteger[]OptionalUp to 50 transaction ids to dispute instead of the defaults above. Each must be one of the transactions the subject allows
reasonstringRequired3 to 255 characters
descriptionstringOptionalUp to 2000 characters

Request

POST /api/v1/complaints HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "customer_id": 42,
  "game_wallet_id": 1051,
  "reason": "The winner kept playing after I disconnected"
}

Response (201)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": 3, "complaint_id": "9b1c2f0e-6a57-4f7e-9d0a-1f3c5b8e2a41", "customer_id": 42,
    "subject_type": "game", "game_wallet_id": 1051, "competition_wallet_id": null,
    "reason": "The winner kept playing after I disconnected", "description": null,
    "status": "pending_dispute", "disputed_amount": 190.00, "held_amount": 150.00, "shortfall_amount": 40.00,
    "refunded_amount": 0.00, "house_cuts_reversed": 0.00, "released_amount": 0.00,
    "filed_by": "api_key:4", "resolution_note": null, "closed_by": null, "closed_at": null,
    "created_at": "2026-09-23T14:05:00+03:00",
    "disputed_transactions": [
      {
        "id": 5, "transaction_type": "game_transaction", "transaction_id": 88213, "customer_id": 57,
        "source_wallet_type": "wallet", "source_wallet_id": 57,
        "amount": 190.00, "held_amount": 150.00, "shortfall_amount": 40.00, "balance": 150.00, "status": "held"
      }
    ],
    "refunds": []
  }
}

Notes

transaction_type is game_transaction or competition_transaction. source_wallet_type is wallet (a customer wallet) or competition_wallet. balance is what is still held for that transaction. refunded_amount, house_cuts_reversed, released_amount and refunds are filled in when the complaint is closed (see below). A repeated identical request replays the earlier response (see Idempotency).

Error Responses

// 422 - validation, e.g. both subjects sent
{"message": "The game wallet id field prohibits competition wallet id from being present.", "errors": {...}}
// 422 - the complaint cannot be filed
{"success": false, "message": "Only a player in this game can complain about it."}
{"success": false, "message": "This game has no winner payout to dispute."}
{"success": false, "message": "This competition wallet has no win to dispute."}
{"success": false, "message": "A customer cannot complain about their own competition wallet."}
{"success": false, "message": "transaction_ids must be winner payouts in this game. Not found: 88100."}
// 409 - a transaction is already under an open complaint
{"success": false, "message": "Transaction 88213 is already under an open complaint."}
GET/api/v1/complaintsList complaints, newest first▶
API Key

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
statusstringOptionalpending_dispute, resolved, rejected or cancelled
subject_typestringOptionalgame, tournament or jackpot
customer_idintegerOptionalThe complainant
game_wallet_idintegerOptionalComplaints about this game
competition_wallet_idintegerOptionalComplaints about this competition wallet
from / todate (Y-m-d)OptionalFiled on or after / on or before
page / per_pageintegerOptionalper_page defaults to 50, maximum 200

Response (200)

{
  "data": [ { "id": 3, "status": "pending_dispute", "subject_type": "game", "disputed_amount": 190.00, "disputed_transactions": [ ... ], ... } ],
  "links": { "first": "...", "last": "...", "prev": null, "next": null },
  "meta": { "current_page": 1, "per_page": 50, "total": 1, ... }
}
GET/api/v1/complaints/{encryptedIdentifier}One complaint with its disputed transactions▶
API KeyEncrypted ID

Response (200)

{"success": true, "data": { "id": 3, "complaint_id": "9b1c2f0e-...", "status": "pending_dispute", "disputed_transactions": [ ... ], ... }}

Error Responses

{"success": false, "message": "Complaint not found"}
POST/api/v1/complaints/{encryptedIdentifier}/resolveThe complaint is valid: reverse the disputed transactions and refund the players▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min

Description

Every disputed transaction on the complaint is reversed together, and refunds are always paid to the players' main (customer) wallets:

SubjectWho is refundedPaid from
GameEvery player gets back their stake in the game (deposits minus any refund they already had), including the winnerThe held money plus every house cut taken on the game, which is reversed (ledger house_cut_reversal)
Tournament or jackpotOnly the disputed rounds are reversed. A disputed win goes back to the player who lost that round; a disputed deposit to the player who placed it; a disputed payout to the losers of every round the wallet won, with any remainder (the winner's own stake) back to the winnerThe held money only. Competition house cuts are not reversed

When there was a shortfall, players share what there is in proportion to what each is owed, so nobody is refunded in full. Amounts are split in whole cents; leftover cents go to the largest claims. Reversed house cuts reduce revenue in the income statement on the day of the reversal. A frozen competition wallet is unfrozen.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
notestringRequired3 to 255 characters, saved as resolution_note

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": 3, "status": "resolved", "subject_type": "game",
    "disputed_amount": 180.00, "held_amount": 180.00, "shortfall_amount": 0.00,
    "refunded_amount": 200.00, "house_cuts_reversed": 20.00, "released_amount": 0.00,
    "resolution_note": "Winner used a bot", "closed_by": "api_key:4", "closed_at": "2026-09-24T09:30:00+03:00",
    "disputed_transactions": [ { "id": 5, "status": "reversed", "balance": 0.00, ... } ],
    "refunds": [
      {"customer_id": 42, "wallet_id": 42, "amount": 100.00},
      {"customer_id": 57, "wallet_id": 57, "amount": 100.00}
    ],
    ...
  }
}

Error Responses

// 404
{"success": false, "message": "Complaint not found"}
// 409 - already closed
{"success": false, "message": "Complaint is already rejected."}
// 422 - missing note, or the losing round of a disputed win cannot be found
{"success": false, "message": "The losing round for win 7712 was not found."}

A customer shares their own referral code (the client generates the code, link and QR code). A new customer who signs up with it (referral_code on POST /customers) becomes their referral. The referrer earns KES 10 when the client reports the referral verified (email and phone), and KES 10 on the referral's first wallet deposit of any amount (an M-Pesa deposit or a cash load at a bet shop, whichever comes first), paid once they are verified. Coin loads, gifts and emoji are not deposits. Each milestone pays once. Bonuses go to a separate referral wallet, which the customer can withdraw to M-Pesa (minimum KES 50, whole shillings) from the referral shortcode. Only signups on or after REFERRALS_EFFECTIVE_FROM count. Codes that belong to no customer (agent codes) are stored on the customer as before and create no referral.

POST/api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/referral-wallet/withdrawWithdraw referral earnings to M-Pesa▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent5/min

Description

Pays the amount to the customer's phone number from the referral shortcode (not the main B2C shortcode). The referral wallet is debited straight away (ledger entry referral_withdrawal) and the B2C request is sent. processing means Safaricom accepted it; the result callback then marks it completed, or failed and credits the wallet back (referral_withdrawal_reversal). If Safaricom rejects the request outright the money is returned at once (502). If the outcome is unknown (network error) the withdrawal stays pending and is not refunded automatically, because it may still be paid; finance reconciles it. The house pays the B2C fee.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
amountintegerRequiredWhole shillings, at least 50 and no more than the referral wallet balance

Request

POST /api/v1/customers/{encryptedIdentifier}/referral-wallet/withdraw HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Idempotency-Key: 6f1c...
Content-Type: application/json

{"amount": 100}

Response (201)

{"success": true, "message": "Referral payout sent", "data": {"id": 5, "customer_id": 42, "amount": 100.00, "phone_no": "2547****5678", "status": "processing", "mpesa_receipt": null, "result_code": null, "result_desc": null, "ledger_entry_id": "uuid", "completed_at": null, "failed_at": null, "created_at": "..."}}

Errors

// 400 - insufficient referral wallet balance, or no valid M-Pesa phone number
// 403 - referral withdrawals are switched off
// 422 - below the minimum: {"success": false, "message": "The minimum referral withdrawal is KES 50"}
// 502 - Safaricom rejected the payout; the money is back in the referral wallet (data.status "failed")
// 202 - outcome unknown; kept pending for review (data.status "pending")
// 503 - the referral shortcode is not configured on the server

Since responsible gambling (P4): at most 150,000; the same suspended-player, verified-phone and 5-an-hour rules as POST /api/v1/withdraw/{encryptedIdentifier} (403 player_suspended, 422 phone_unverified, 429 too_many_withdrawals), with the hourly count shared between both wallets. See Responsible Gambling.

  }

How bet shops work. A bet shop is a physical counter where players without M-Pesa or a smartphone pay in cash. It is not a customer and cannot play. Each shop has a prepaid float: the owner buys it by paying the paybill with bill ref SHOP-<shop_no>, and spends it loading players' wallets with the cash they hand over (the owner keeps the cash). While M-Pesa callbacks are not authenticated, those payments are not credited automatically: they land as unmatched deposits carrying the shop in bet_shop, and GMS credits each one with POST /deposits/{id}/shop-float after checking the M-Pesa statement (BET_SHOPS_AUTO_CREDIT_FLOAT turns automatic crediting on later). A float purchase has no excise duty; a cash load does, exactly like an M-Pesa deposit. The shop's system uses its own shop API key (issued below), which only works on the Shop System routes and always acts for that shop. Shop status: active; suspended (its keys are refused, the float is kept); closed (final, only once the float is empty, e.g. by recording a float redemption; its keys are revoked). Only GMS can reverse a load: a shop can never take money back from a player. The game server sends bet_shop_no on POST /game/bets and POST /competition/transactions for play at a shop desktop; those stakes and entries carry bet_shop_id and bet_shop_no (both null for online play) in GET /game/bets and GET /competition/transactions.

POST/api/v1/bet-shops/{encryptedIdentifier}/keysIssue a key for the shop's system▶
API KeyEncrypted ID30/min

Description

Returns the new key in key this once: store it on the shop's desktop now. The key only works on the Shop System routes and always acts for this shop. Not idempotent on purpose: no Idempotency-Key is read or derived here, so the response is never stored, and request logs keep only a hash of the request, never the key. A retry after a timeout issues a second key: list the shop's keys and revoke the extra one. Refused for a closed shop (409 shop_closed).

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredThe shop: an encrypted bet shop id

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription

Check the payment on the M-Pesa statement first: callbacks are not authenticated yet, so a deposit can be forged. The full amount goes to the shop's float: no excise duty, no referral. POST/api/v1/bet-shops/float-redemptions/{encryptedIdentifier}/voidVoid a float redemption▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min

Description

For one recorded by mistake, or a payment that did not go through: the amount goes back on the float. Refused when already voided (409 already_resolved).

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredThe redemption: an encrypted id

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
reasonstringRequired3 to 255 characters

Response (200)

{"success": true, "message": "Float redemption voided; the amount is back on the float", "data": { ..., "status": "voided" }}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 404, 422: see Error Responses.

Cash withdrawals at a shop. Only the player can ask for one, from the client while logged in: the amount leaves their wallet at once and is held on the withdrawal, and they get a 6-digit pickup code shown only to them, only once. At the counter they give the code; the shop enters it, hands over the cash from its till, and the amount goes to the shop's float. A request that is not collected within 24 hours expires and the money goes back to the wallet (the scheduler runs shop-withdrawals:expire every 5 minutes); so does one the player cancels, one the shop rejects (e.g. not enough cash in the till), and one that gets 5 wrong codes (so a shop cannot guess its way in). KES 50 to 20,000, whole shillings. The player needs an ID number on the account (id_no), and locked signup bonus cannot be withdrawn. While pending, the money is owed to the player: it shows as shop_withdrawals on the balance sheet and the customer cannot be deleted.

GET/api/v1/bet-shops/{encryptedIdentifier}/withdrawalsA shop's cash withdrawals▶
API KeyEncrypted ID

Description

The same as GET /bet-shops/withdrawals for one shop.

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredThe shop: an encrypted bet shop id

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
statusstringOptionalpending, paid, cancelled, rejected or expired
customer_idintegerOptionalOne player's withdrawals
from, todateOptionalY-m-d, on the day the withdrawal was asked for (Nairobi time); to not before from
pageintegerOptionalDefault 1
per_pageintegerOptional1 to 200, default 50

Response (200)

{"success": true, "data": {"items": [ ... ], "pagination": { ... }}}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 404: see Error Responses.

For the shop's desktop system. Every request uses the shop's own API key (issued by GMS under Bet Shops) in X-API-KEY. A shop key works only on these routes (403 anywhere else), and always acts for its own shop: the shop is never taken from the request. A suspended or closed shop gets 403 shop_not_active. Limits per key: 60 requests a minute, of which 30 player lookups. Loading a player: ask for their 8-digit player number (shown to them in the app, e.g. 1234 5678), look it up, confirm the masked name with them, take the cash, then load. Each load needs a new Idempotency-Key; when a load times out, retry it with the same key and it is never loaded twice. The player receives the amount less 5% excise duty (KES 1,000 cash: KES 950 in the wallet), the shop float goes down by the full amount, and the shop keeps the cash. A load is whole shillings, KES 50 to 20,000, and one player can be loaded at most KES 50,000 a day across all shops. A mistake is reversed by Kadi Kings, never by the shop. Paying out cash: a player asks for a withdrawal in the app and gets a pickup code. It appears in GET /shop/withdrawals; at the counter, take the code from the player, pay it with POST /shop/withdrawals/{id}/pay, and hand over the cash only after a 200. The amount is added to your float.

POST/api/v1/shop/loadsLoad a player with cash▶
API KeyIdempotent

Description

Requires an Idempotency-Key header (400 idempotency_key_required without one): a new key for each load, the same key to retry one. A retry returns the first load (its load_id) and nothing is loaded again. Refusals carry code: invalid_player_no (422), player_not_found (404), player_inactive (409), insufficient_float (409, with float_balance), daily_limit_reached (422, with what is left today).

Since responsible gambling (P4): a player on a break gets 403 player_excluded, and a load above what the player's deposit limits leave gets 422 deposit_limit with remaining. Nothing is loaded. See Responsible Gambling.

POST/api/v1/shop/withdrawals/{withdrawalId}/payPay a player their cash▶
API KeyIdempotent

Description

Enter the pickup code the player gives you. When it is right the request is paid: hand over amount in cash, and the amount is added to the shop float. Requires an Idempotency-Key (400 idempotency_key_required without one): a new key for each attempt, the same key only to retry one that timed out. A wrong code returns 422 wrong_code with attempts_left; the 5th wrong code cancels the request and gives the money back to the player (409 too_many_attempts). Also 409 not_pending (already paid, cancelled, rejected or expired) and 409 expired (past its 24 hours: the money went back). Only this shop's requests (404 otherwise).

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
withdrawalIdstringRequiredThe withdrawal's withdrawal_id (a UUID, not encrypted)

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
pickup_codestringRequiredThe 6-digit code from the player

Request

POST /api/v1/shop/withdrawals/9d2f6c1e-3b7a-4c55-8e0d-2a1f4b6c7d8e/pay HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke

POST /api/v1/load/{encryptedIdentifier} STK Push for a gift or emoji ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent5/min
201 Created 404 Not Found 422 Unprocessable 500 Server Error

Prompts a phone to pay amount. The account reference tells KadiApi what the payment buys when Safaricom confirms it: {account_no}#{amount}#gift or {account_no}#{amount}#emoji, with #{referral_code} appended when there is one. A gift or emoji is recorded as a purchase and never credits the wallet. To top up the wallet use POST /api/v1/deposits/{encryptedIdentifier}. The old load type is gone: a paybill payment whose account reference ends in #load is credited as an ordinary deposit of the amount paid. 201 only means the prompt was sent. Validation failures return 422; M-Pesa errors return 500.

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredThe customer: encrypted customer id or account number

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
amountnumberRequiredKES to charge, greater than 0
typestringRequiredgift or emoji
phone_nostringOptionalPhone to prompt instead of the customer's own (for example someone buying a gift)
referral_codestringOptionalAgent code to attach; defaults to the customer's stored referral_code

Example Request

POST /api/v1/load/{encryptedIdentifier} HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Idempotency-Key: 6e8a0c2e-4b6d-4f81-a3c5-e7092b4d6f81
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "amount": 50,
  "type": "gift",
  "phone_no": "254712345678"
}

Response (201: prompt sent)

{
  "status": "success"
}

Response (404)

{
  "message": "Customer not found"
}

Response (422)

{
  "message": "The selected type is invalid.",
  "errors": {
    "type": ["The selected type is invalid."]
  }
}

  "message": "Could not start the M-Pesa payment, please try again"
}

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

What the finance endpoints are

Everything under /api/v1/finance/ reports on money: income, cash flow, what is owed to customers, and where the numbers come from. All endpoints are read-only GET requests except recording and voiding expenses and excise duty remittances. They all need the X-API-KEY header.

GroupEndpoints
Financial Statementssummary, income-statement, house-banked, cash-flow, balance-sheet, trial-balance, reconciliation, taxes
Drill-downsdeposits, withdrawals, purchases, games, competitions, ledger, adjustments, disputes, referrals (summary, bonuses, withdrawals), customers/top, customer statement
Expensesrecord, void and list expenses
Excise Dutyexcise-duty, excise-duty/charges, excise-duty/returns, excise-duty/remittances (list, record, void)
Winnings Taxwinnings-tax, winnings-tax/charges, winnings-tax/returns, winnings-tax/remittances (list, record, void)
CSV Exportany report as a CSV download

Common query parameters

All optional. They apply to every finance GET unless an endpoint says otherwise.

ParameterTypeDescription
fromdate (Y-m-d)First day, inclusive. Default: today (last 30 days for reconciliation, customers/top and the customer statement)
todate (Y-m-d)Last day, inclusive. Must not be before from. The range may not exceed 366 days
group_bystringday (default), week (weeks start Monday) or month. Used by reports that return a series
exclude_testbooleanDefault true. Leaves out test customers. Accepts 1, 0, true, false
page, per_pageintegerLists only. Page 1, 50 per page by default, at most 200 per page
product, gamestringOnly reports built from ledger entries take them: ledger, adjustments, customers/top, trial-balance, income-statement, house-banked, winnings-tax/charges and their CSV exports (plus income-by-product). product is a product code (e.g. kadi, crash_classic), game a game code meaning every product of that game. An unknown code, or a product of another game, is a 422 validation error. Every other finance report covers the whole platform (cash, deposits, withdrawals, excise duty, referrals, bet shops, expenses, balances, the customer statement and the summary) and answers 422 {"success": false, "code": "product_filter_not_supported"}

Response format

Successful responses use the same envelope as the statistics endpoints. Reports over a range include a meta block; every report includes generated_at.

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "generated_at": "2026-09-20T12:00:00+03:00",
    "meta": {
      "period": {"from": "2026-09-01", "to": "2026-09-20", "group_by": "day", "exclude_test": true},
      "ledger_started_at": "2026-09-01",
      "warnings": []
    }
    /* report fields */
  }
}

Lists add summary (totals over everything the filters match, not just the current page), items and pagination:

"pagination": {"page": 1, "per_page": 50, "total": 132, "last_page": 3}

Money is a number in KES rounded to 2 decimals. Validation errors return 422; see Error Responses.

Dates and time zone

Days are Nairobi days (Africa/Nairobi). A range covers whole days from 00:00 on from to 23:59:59 on to. "Today" ends at Nairobi midnight, not UTC midnight.

Ledger start date and warnings

Revenue, stakes, payouts and balances come from the ledger, which began recording on 2026-09-01. Cash in and cash out come from payment records and cover earlier dates. When a range starts before the ledger began, meta.warnings says which figures are zero before that date.

Escrow releases (game and competition payouts leaving their wallets) were only ledgered from a later date, so the trial balance and escrow checks may not balance before it. The warning appears in meta.warnings too.

Test customers

Reports can leave out test customers listed on the server in FINANCE_TEST_CUSTOMER_IDS. There are none by default, so in production every customer is included. When some are listed, reports leave them out unless you send exclude_test=0. The house wallet is always kept apart from customer wallets, whatever this list says. House cuts cannot always be tied to a player, so the income statement only removes cuts it can link to a test customer or a game a test customer played.

Caching and rate limits

Reports whose range includes today are cached for about 2 minutes, so a figure can lag by that long. Closed ranges and past balance sheets are cached for an hour. The drill-down lists are not cached. Finance GET endpoints share the stats limit of 20 requests per minute; recording or voiding an expense or an excise duty remittance uses the write limit of 30 per minute.

Privacy

Phone numbers are masked in JSON and CSV output, for example 254712345678 becomes 2547****5678. This includes phone-like values inside account references. Customer names are shown; use customer_id to look a customer up elsewhere.

House fee schedule

WhereRate
Game credit, older stakes (before POST /game/credit became the voided game share)5% of the stake, taken when it was credited
Voided game share (POST /game/credit)what is left of the pot after the game server's shares (about 5%)
Game payout (POST /game/withdraw)a further 5% of the pool, rounded down
Dropped game (POST /game/drop)10% of the dropped players' stakes, rounded up
Tournament entry10%
Jackpot entry20%

Finance reports show what was actually taken; the reconciliation report flags cuts that differ from this schedule.

Manual balance changes are audited

Changing a wallet balance by hand (deposit, withdraw, balance, wallet update and the customer wallet update) is recorded in the ledger as an adjustment with an optional reason and the API key that made it. See GET /finance/adjustments.

See Finance Overview for the common parameters, response format and how dates work. All endpoints are rate-limited to 20 requests/minute.

GET/api/v1/finance/income-statementRevenue by stream, less expenses, as a series▶
API Key20/min

Description

Revenue is house cuts as they are booked in the ledger, plus gift and emoji sales at the cash received. Tournament and jackpot cuts are split by rounds where the cut is linked to its competition entry; older cuts appear as competitions_unattributed. expenses are the active entries recorded through /finance/expenses, dated by their expense date. referral_payouts are referral withdrawals completed in the period: the referral programme expense (earned bonuses cost nothing until they are paid out; they are shown in memo.referral_bonuses_earned). promotions is the gross cost of signup bonuses granted in the period: what the house wallet paid, including the excise duty owed on it. revenue.house_banked is the GGR of house-banked products (sportsbook, crash, slots, scratch cards): stakes settled to the house minus payouts, both net of reversals, negative when players won more than they lost. wager_promotions is what bonuses and free-bet winnings on wagers cost the house. net_income is revenue minus expenses, referral payouts, promotions and wager promotions, before tax (see /finance/taxes). memo.winnings_tax_withheld is tax withheld from house-banked wins: owed to KRA, not revenue. by_product has one row per product (net = revenue minus promotions) and a platform row for everything with no product (gift and emoji sales, house cuts not tagged with a product, signup promotions, referral payouts, expenses), which is not allocated; the rows add up to net_income. A house-banked product's row also has turnover, payouts and rtp (see /finance/house-banked); they are null for rake products. With product or game, only that product's ledger lines count, platform lines are 0 and memo.load_margin is null. memo.load_margin is cash received for wallet loads minus the wallet credit given; it is shown for information and is not part of revenue. House cuts reversed when a complaint is resolved reduce the same revenue line on the day they are reversed.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
from, to, group_by, exclude_test OptionalSee common parameters
product, gamestringOptionalOne product code, or every product of one game code. See common parameters

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "generated_at": "2026-09-20T12:00:00+03:00",

GET/api/v1/finance/house-bankedBets, turnover, payouts, RTP and hold of house-banked games▶
API Key20/min

Description

How the house-banked games (slots, scratch cards, sportsbook, crash) performed, per product and per period. bets are wagers settled won or lost (a free bet is a bet) and voids wagers settled void. turnover is cash stakes settled to the house; free bets are not turnover, their face value is free_bet_value. payouts are cash winnings paid by the house before winnings tax. ggr = turnover − payouts and equals the income statement's revenue.house_banked. rtp (return to player) is payouts as a percentage of turnover and hold is ggr as a percentage of turnover; both are null when nothing was staked. bonuses and free_bet_winnings are promotion costs (together the income statement's wager_promotions), and net = ggr − bonuses − free_bet_winnings, a product's by_product net in the income statement. Everything is net of reversals: a reversed settlement is taken off in the period it was reversed, so a period can show negative figures. players is the number of distinct players with a won or lost bet in the range whose settlement still stands today, so unlike the other figures it changes when an old settlement is reversed; it is in totals and by_product but not in series. Tombstones (a void for a reference that was never placed) are not counted. winnings_tax_withheld is owed to KRA, see /finance/winnings-tax.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
from, to, group_by, exclude_test OptionalSee common parameters
product, gamestringOptionalOne product code, or every product of one game code. See common parameters

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "generated_at": "2026-10-31T12:00:00+03:00",
    "meta": {"period": {"from": "2026-10-31", "to": "2026-10-31", "group_by": "day", "exclude_test": true}, "ledger_started_at": "2026-09-01", "warnings": []},

GET/api/v1/finance/cash-flowM-Pesa cash in by what it bought, cash out by status▶
API Key20/min

Description

Cash in is what customers actually paid (incoming payments), split into wallet_deposit, load, gift, emoji, unmatched (money received from an unknown customer and not credited to anyone; it is included in the total) house_funding (owner money credited to the house wallet: capital, not customer deposits) and shop_float (float bet shop owners bought through the paybill; the cash players later pay at the shops stays with the owners, so shop loads are not cash in). Cash out is outgoing payments by status: paid, pending, failed. excise_duty.withheld is the excise duty charged in the period: taken from deposits (already inside cash in) and owed on signup bonuses and excise_duty.remitted is what was paid to KRA in the period, by payment date. referral_payouts are referral withdrawals from the referral shortcode: paid by completion date, failed by failure date, pending (still open) by request date. deposit_refunds.refunded is unmatched deposits recorded as sent back to the payer, by the day the refund was recorded (the deposit stays in cash in as unmatched, the day it arrived). shop_float.redeemed is float paid back to bet shop owners (recorded by GMS). shop_cash is cash that changed hands at shop counters: loaded (paid in by players, gross) and paid_out (paid out to them, by payment day); it never reaches M-Pesa, so it is not in net cash. net_cash is cash in minus paid withdrawals minus excise duty remitted minus paid referral payouts minus deposit refunds minus float redeemed. Works for dates before the ledger began.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
from, to, group_by, exclude_test OptionalSee common parameters

Response (200)

      "cash_out": {"paid": 150.00, "pending": 40.00, "failed": 60.00},

GET/api/v1/finance/balance-sheetCash held against what is owed, live or as at a past day▶
API Key20/min

Description

Assets are the latest balance of every M-Pesa account (from the hourly balance fetch), listed individually. Liabilities are what is owed: customer wallets (test customers and the house wallet excluded), open game and competition wallets, stuck_escrow (money left in finished wallets), coin liability, pending holds, unmatched_deposits and excise_duty_payable (excise duty taken from deposits and not yet in a recorded KRA remittance; snapshots from before it was tracked show 0) and winnings_tax_payable (tax withheld from house-banked wins less credits for reversed wins, not yet in a recorded KRA remittance; negative while credits exceed charges; snapshots from before it was tracked show 0) and disputed_funds (winnings held while a complaint is pending; still owed to a customer, snapshots from before complaints existed show 0) and referral_wallets (referral bonuses not yet withdrawn; customers can withdraw them at any time, snapshots from before referrals existed show 0) and bet_shop_floats (float bet shop owners have bought and not spent; owed back to them if redeemed) and shop_withdrawals (cash withdrawals at bet shops waiting to be collected; still owed to the player) and open_wagers (stakes held in the escrow of house-banked bets not yet settled; snapshots from before it was tracked show 0). by_product splits what belongs to a product (open wagers, game and competition escrow, winnings tax payable) and lists the rest under platform; customer wallets are one platform line, since one wallet serves every game. For a past as_of it is read from that day's snapshot lines and is empty for snapshots taken before they existed. Snapshots from before bet shops existed show 0 for both. The referral shortcode's balance is listed among the M-Pesa accounts as type referral_b2c once its balance URLs are set. house_wallet is shown separately. difference is cash minus liabilities minus the house wallet. It is not expected to be zero: it also holds gift and emoji sales not yet moved to the house wallet, M-Pesa charges, and timing between the hourly balance fetch and wallet movements. A daily snapshot is taken at 23:59 Nairobi time; as_of reads that day's snapshot.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
as_ofdate (Y-m-d)OptionalNot in the future. Returns that day's snapshot. 404 if there is none. Omit for the live position

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "generated_at": "2026-09-20T12:00:00+03:00",
    "as_of": "2026-09-20",
    "source": "live",
    "assets": {
      "cash": {
        "accounts": [
          {"type": "b2c", "account": "Utility Account", "amount": 1000.00, "as_of": "2026-09-20T11:00:00+03:00"},
          {"type": "c2b", "account": "Working Account", "amount": 2500.00, "as_of": "2026-09-20T11:00:00+03:00"}
        ],
        "total": 3500.00
      },
      "total": 3500.00
    },
    "liabilities": {
      "customer_wallets": 2000.00, "game_escrow": 40.00, "competition_escrow": 0.00, "stuck_escrow": 0.00,
      "coin_liability": 0.00, "pending_holds": 0.00, "unmatched_deposits": 75.00, "excise_duty_payable": 25.00, "disputed_funds": 0.00, "referral_wallets": 0.00, "bet_shop_floats": 0.00, "shop_withdrawals": 0.00, "total": 2140.00
    },
    "house_wallet": 300.00,
    "difference": 1060.00,
    "notes": []
  }
}

Error Responses

GET/api/v1/finance/trial-balanceLedger movement by account, with a balance check▶
API Key20/min

Description

Debits and credits per account and entry type. Deposits, withdrawals, manual adjustments, excise duty (category tax_withheld, owed to KRA), referral bonuses, referral payouts, house funding and bet shop float purchases and redemptions (shop_float_purchase, shop_float_redemption, category shop_float) are single-sided in the ledger (the cash side is M-Pesa or the referral shortcode); every other entry is paired and must net to zero. A bet shop cash load (shop_load) is paired: the shop float pays what the player's wallet receives. So are shop cash withdrawals: held out of the wallet (shop_withdrawal_hold), given back (shop_withdrawal_release) or paid into the shop float (shop_cash_out). Signup bonuses (promo_credit, category promotion) are paired: the house wallet pays what the customer wallet receives. check.balanced says whether it does, check.imbalance is the amount left over, and imbalance_by_category shows where it comes from. Coin entries are in coins, not KES, and are left out. exclude_test does not apply, because the check only holds over the whole ledger.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
from, todateOptionalSee common parameters
product, gamestringOptionalOnly that product's entries. check.balanced is then null: a product slice leaves out the platform side of the money (deposits, withdrawals), so it does not have to net to zero

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "generated_at": "2026-09-20T12:00:00+03:00",
    "meta": {},
    "lines": [
      {"account": "customer_wallets", "entry_type": "deposit", "category": "cash_in", "unit": "KES", "debit": 0.00, "credit": 500.00, "entries": 1},
      {"account": "house_wallet", "entry_type": "house_cut", "category": "house_revenue", "unit": "KES", "debit": 0.00, "credit": 50.00, "entries": 6}
    ],
    "totals": {"debit": 640.00, "credit": 1090.00},
    "check": {"balanced": true, "imbalance": 0.00, "imbalance_by_category": []},
    "notes": []
  }
}
GET/api/v1/finance/reconciliationPass/warn/fail controls over the ledger, wallets, payments, excise duty and bet shops▶
API Key20/min

Description

Runs the checks below. Balance checks look at current state; checks that scan entries or payments use the range (the last 30 days when from is not sent). Each check has a status (pass, warn or fail), a count of items, an amount of money involved and up to 10 samples. The overall status is the worst of them.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
from, todateOptionalDefault: last 30 days

Checks

KeyStatus when it tripsWhat it checks
customer_wallet_driftfailCustomer wallet balances match opening balance plus ledger credits minus debits
game_wallet_driftfailSame for game wallets (from the day escrow releases were ledgered)
competition_wallet_driftfailSame for competition wallets
dispute_escrow_driftfailSame for money held in dispute escrow by complaints
referral_wallet_driftfailSame for referral wallets
bet_shop_float_driftfailSame for bet shop floats
shop_withdrawal_driftfailSame for shop cash withdrawals waiting to be collected
ledger_arithmeticfailEvery ledger entry adds up: before + credit - debit = after
ledger_balancefailPaired ledger entries net to zero (see trial balance)
unclassified_entrieswarnLedger entries that have no wallet type
unmatched_depositswarnMoney received from unknown customers and not credited (resolve with assign or refund)
deposit_resolutionsfailResolved unmatched deposits agree with their records: assigned ones are completed with a ledger credit, house-funded ones with a house_funding credit, shop float ones with a shop_float_purchase credit, refunded ones have status 4 and a refund record
deposits_without_ledgerfailProcessed deposits that never reached the ledger (gift and emoji purchases are not expected to)
deposits_without_excise_dutyfailPlain wallet deposits credited in full while excise duty was on (from its start date). Passes with a note when excise duty is off
excise_duty_amountsfailEach charge is 5% of the gross rounded to the cent, and gross = excise + net
excise_duty_ledgerfailEach charge matches its excise_duty ledger entry: same amount, and reversed only when the charge was reversed
overdue_excise_dutywarnMonths whose excise duty is still unpaid after the 20th of the next month
winnings_tax_amountsfailWinnings tax rows that differ from their settlement's tax or from round(base x rate), or carry the wrong sign for their kind
winnings_tax_ledgerfailWinnings tax rows whose ledger entry is missing, of the wrong type, or for a different amount
settlements_without_winnings_taxfailSettlements that withheld tax with no charge row, or reversed ones with no credit row
overdue_winnings_taxwarnMonths whose winnings tax is still unpaid after the filing day of the next month
stuck_withdrawalswarnWithdrawals pending longer than 24 hours
failed_withdrawals_not_reversedfailA payout failed but the customer was not refunded
stuck_escrowwarnMoney left in game or competition wallets that are no longer open
aged_escrowwarnOpen game or competition wallets holding money, untouched for over 24 hours
negative_balancesfailAny wallet (customer, game, competition, referral, bet shop float or shop withdrawal) with a negative balance, except a player made negative by a wager reversal and the house wallet, which have their own checks
negative_balances_from_reversalwarnPlayers whose wallet went below zero through a wager settlement reversal (expected: the player owes it, the next credits repay it, stakes and withdrawals are blocked until then)
negative_house_balancewarnThe house wallet is below zero (house-banked wins can do this; top it up with house funding)
wager_escrow_driftfailHouse-banked wagers whose escrow balance does not match their ledger entries
aged_open_wagerswarnWagers still open after their product's threshold (30 days by default, FINANCE_OPEN_WAGER_DAYS, overridable per product in config)
settled_wagers_with_balancefailWon, lost or void wagers whose escrow is not empty
held_on_closed_complaintsfailMoney still in dispute escrow for a complaint that is resolved, rejected or cancelled
aged_disputeswarnComplaints still pending after 3 days (FINANCE_AGED_DISPUTE_DAYS), with the money they hold
stuck_referral_withdrawalswarnReferral withdrawals still pending or processing after 24 hours. Check the payout on the referral shortcode before doing anything: a missing result does not mean it was not paid
failed_referral_withdrawals_not_reversedfailA referral payout failed but the referral wallet was not credited back
referral_bonuses_unverifiedfailReferral bonuses paid for referrals that are not verified
house_funding_ledgerfailEach house funding has its credit to the house wallet for the full deposit and its deposit completed, and each voided one has its reversal
shop_float_ledgerfailEach bet shop float purchase has its credit to that shop's float for the full deposit and its deposit completed, and each voided one has its reversal
shop_loads_ledgerfailEach bet shop cash load moved its gross amount from the shop float to the player's wallet (a matching pair of shop_load entries), and gross = excise + net
stale_shop_withdrawalswarnShop cash withdrawals more than an hour past their expiry still holding the money (the shop-withdrawals:expire job is not running)
held_on_closed_shop_withdrawalsfailA paid, cancelled, rejected or expired shop withdrawal still holding money
promotion_credits_ledgerfailEach signup bonus has its customer credit and house debit in the ledger for the gross amount, and its excise duty charge when duty was taken
house_cut_rateswarnHouse cuts that differ from the fee schedule (only cuts linked to their transaction can be checked)
mpesa_balance_freshnesswarnM-Pesa balances fetched in the last 3 hours
cash_coveragefailM-Pesa cash covers everything owed to customers (including unspent referral balances), bet shop floats, shop withdrawals waiting to be collected and the excise duty owed to KRA

Response (200)

  "success": true,

GET/api/v1/finance/taxesExcise duty charged, estimated other taxes and net income after tax▶
API Key20/min

Description

excise_duty is the actual duty taken from deposits in the period, net of reversals (actual: true); for what is due and paid each month use /finance/excise-duty/returns. The other lines apply the configured rate of each tax to its base (actual: false). Those rates are off (0) until the server is configured, and configured is false until a rate is set or excise duty is on; the bases are still shown. The estimates are for planning, not tax returns. Confirm rates and which taxes apply with your accountant or tax adviser.

TaxBaseKind
excise_dutygross wallet deposits charged in the period (actual duty, not an estimate)pass_through (taken from customers and paid to KRA, not a cost)
withholding_taxwinnings paid to customerspass_through (collected and passed on, not a cost)
income_taxnet income before tax, less any expense taxes above, never below zeroexpense

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
from, to, exclude_test OptionalSee common parameters. Stakes and winnings cover the period since the ledger began

All lists take from, to, exclude_test, page and per_page (default 50, maximum 200) as described in Finance Overview. Each list returns a summary covering everything the filters match. All endpoints are rate-limited to 20 requests/minute, and every list can be downloaded as CSV from /finance/export/{report}.

GET/api/v1/finance/gamesOne row per game: stakes, payouts, refunds, outcome and house take▶
API Key20/min

Description

Games are dated by when the game wallet was created. house_take is stakes minus what players were paid and refunded minus anything still in the game wallet, so it is only shown once the game is closed (null while open). It includes both the cut taken at credit and the cut taken at payout. A negative take means players were paid or refunded more than they staked. players counts the customers who staked; a stake whose customer was deleted before customers were soft-deleted counts as one player of its own. With exclude_test on, any game a test customer staked in is left out.

OutcomeMeaning
openThe game wallet is still open
completedClosed and a player was paid
droppedClosed after a drop-connection payout or refund
refundedClosed with refunds and no payout
closed_emptyClosed with no payout and no refund

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
outcomestringOptionalOne of the outcomes above
game_typeintegerOptionalGame type
playersintegerOptionalGames with exactly this many players

Response (200)

      "by_players": {"1": {"games": 1, "stakes": 100.00, "paid_to_players": 0.00, "refunded": 0.00, "house_take": 0.00}, "2": {"games": 1, "stakes": 200.00, "paid_to_players": 180.00, "refunded": 0.00, "house_take": 20.00}}
    },

GET/api/v1/finance/competitionsOne row per competition: entries, house cut, pool, prizes and what is held▶
API Key20/min

Description

One row per competition (cmp_uid), all players' wallets together, dated by when the first wallet was created. pool is entries minus the house cut; unaccounted is pool minus prizes paid minus money still held in open wallets, which should be near zero for a finished competition. The house cut is only known for entries linked to their ledger cut, so older competitions show a cut of 0 and the whole entry as pool. With exclude_test on, competitions a test customer played in are left out.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
game_typeintegerOptional1 tournament, 2 jackpot
jp_roundsintegerOptionalNumber of rounds

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "data": {

GET/api/v1/finance/open-walletsKadi: open game and competition wallets left untouched, money that may be stranded▶
API Key20/min

Description

A position as of now: every open (status 1) game wallet and competition wallet nothing has happened on for a while. from and to are not applied; exclude_test is (on by default, leaving out games a test customer staked in and competition wallets a test customer owns). A wallet's last_activity_at is its newest transaction (stake, entry, round, payout), or its creation when it has none; it is listed once that is at least the idle threshold old: 30 minutes for single games and 24 hours for tournaments and jackpots by default (FINANCE_OPEN_GAME_IDLE_MINUTES, FINANCE_OPEN_COMPETITION_IDLE_MINUTES), or idle_minutes for both when sent. Rows are ordered longest idle first.

One row per game wallet, with every player who staked in it (participants, each with their total stake), and one per competition wallet (each competition player has their own, so participants is its owner and their entries). players counts the customers who staked in a game; a stake whose customer was deleted before customers were soft-deleted has no customer left and counts as one player of its own (customer_id and name null). under_complaint is true while a pending complaint holds the wallet. The summary groups single games by players, always listing 1 to 4 (1-player games are stakes still waiting for an opponent), tournaments by rounds (3, 4, 5) and jackpots by rounds (13, 17, 21), plus any other value found; held is the money in the wallets.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
kindstringOptionalgame, tournament or jackpot. Without it, all kinds
playersintegerOptionalSingle games with this many players (competition rows have none, so they drop out)
jp_roundsintegerOptionalCompetitions with this many rounds (game rows drop out)
idle_minutesintegerOptional0 to 525600. Replaces both default thresholds; 0 lists every open wallet
exclude_testbooleanOptionalDefault true
page, per_pageintegerOptionalDefault 1 and 50, per_page at most 200

Example Request

GET /api/v1/finance/open-wallets?kind=game&players=1 HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json

GET/api/v1/finance/bet-shopsPer bet shop: float, cash, play and gross gaming revenue▶
API Key20/min

Description

One row per shop (all shops, ordered by shop number). Float bought (float_purchased) and paid back (float_redeemed), cash loads (completed; loads_reversed counts reversed ones) and cash withdrawals paid (cash_outs, by payment day) are for the range; float_balance and withdrawals_pending are as of now. Play is attributed per player and game: a player's stakes in a game count at the shop of their first stake in it (sent as bet_shop_no), with what they won or were refunded from that game, for games whose first such stake falls in the range. game_ggr is stakes in settled games minus those winnings; stakes in games still open are in game_stakes_open and left out until the game is settled. Competitions are attributed per competition wallet the same way: competition_ggr is entries minus prizes paid minus what the wallet still holds. ggr is the two together, and can be negative (players at the shop won more than they staked). Stakes made online are in no shop. With exclude_test on, test customers' cash and play are left out. summary totals the shops listed (every shop, or just the one given by bet_shop_id): shops (how many), the money columns float_balance, float_purchased, float_redeemed, loads_gross, loads_excise, loads_net, cash_out_amount, withdrawals_pending_amount, game_stakes, game_stakes_open, game_winnings, competition_entries, competition_prizes, competition_held, the counts loads, loads_reversed, cash_outs, withdrawals_pending, and game_ggr, competition_ggr and ggr. The CSV export takes the same filters.

Query Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
statusstringOptionalShop status: active, suspended or closed
bet_shop_idintegerOptionalOne shop (the bet_shop_id of a row)
from, todateOptionalSee common parameters
exclude_testbooleanOptionalDefault true
page, per_pageintegerOptionalDefault 1 and 50, per_page at most 200

Response (200)
