POST /api/v1/finance/house-funding/{encryptedIdentifier}/void Void a house funding and reopen its deposit ▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min
200 OK 404 Not Found 409 Conflict 422 Unprocessable

For a payment recorded as house funding by mistake (e.g. a customer's). Takes the amount back out of the house wallet (house_funding_reversal) and puts the deposit back to unmatched (status 0), so it can be assigned or refunded. The funding is kept, marked voided. Refused while the house wallet holds less than the amount (it has been paid out as bonuses).

Path Parameters

ParameterTypeRequiredDescription
encryptedIdentifierstringRequiredEncrypted house funding id

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
reasonstringRequiredWhy, 3 to 255 characters

Example Request

POST /api/v1/finance/house-funding/{encryptedIdentifier}/void HTTP/1.1
Host: api.kadi-kings.co.ke
X-API-KEY: your-api-key-here
Accept: application/json
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "reason": "Customer payment, not owner money"
}

Response (200)

{
  "success": true,
  "message": "House funding voided; the deposit is unmatched again",
  "data": {
    "house_funding": {
      "id": 3,
      "deposit_id": 140,
      "amount": 50000.0,
      "status": "voided",
      "voided_at": "2026-09-26T11:00:00+03:00",
      "voided_by": "api_key:2",
      "void_reason": "Customer payment, not owner money"
    }
  }
}

Response (409: the house wallet has spent it)

{
  "success": false,
  "code": "insufficient_house_balance",
  "message": "The house wallet no longer holds this amount, so it cannot be reversed",
  "data": {
    "house_funding": {"id": 3, "status": "active"}
  }
}

A second void returns 409 with code: already_resolved.

Also returns 401 (API key), 400 (invalid identifier), 409 (Idempotency-Key reused), 429 (rate limit): see Error Responses.

GET/api/v1/finance/customers/topCustomers ranked by activity, and how concentrated wallet balances are▶
API Key20/min

Description

Customer wallet activity from the ledger over the range (default: last 30 days). deposited is everything paid in, gross: M-Pesa deposits plus cash loaded at bet shops (shop_loaded, the shop part, gross and net of reversed loads, so it is not the same as the customer's shop_loads, which is after excise duty). withdrawn is everything taken out: M-Pesa withdrawals plus shop cash withdrawals (shop_withdrawn, collected or waiting to be collected). net_gaming is winnings plus refunds minus stakes; positive means the customer is ahead. balance is the wallet balance now. Not paged: limit caps the list. summary.concentration is the share of all customer wallet balances held by the 10 largest wallets.

Excise duty of 5% is taken from every plain M-Pesa deposit into a customer wallet (C2B confirmation, STK deposits and POST /deposits). A KES 100 deposit is credited in full, then KES 5.00 is debited as excise_duty, leaving KES 95.00. The duty is rounded half up to the cent and there is no minimum deposit. Coin loads, gifts, emoji and deposits from unknown customers are not charged. The duty is not revenue: it is held as excise_duty_payable on the balance sheet until you pay KRA and record a remittance. Returns and payment are due by the 20th of the following month. Excise duty is switched on and dated on the server (FINANCE_EXCISE_DUTY_ENABLED, FINANCE_EXCISE_DUTY_EFFECTIVE_FROM); deposits before the start date are never charged. The rate is saved on each charge.
The summary and charges list follow exclude_test like other reports. The payable amount, monthly returns and remittances always cover every customer, because that is what is owed to KRA. When a deposit is refunded, its excise duty is given back unless it has already been remitted. All GET endpoints are rate-limited to 20 requests/minute and can be downloaded as CSV from /finance/export/{report}.

excise_charged is every charge made in the period, excise_reversed the part later given back, and excise_net what is owed for the period. excise_remitted is what was paid to KRA in the period, by payment date, whatever period the payment covered. payable.outstanding is every charge not yet in a remittance, from all time.

POST/api/v1/finance/excise-duty/remittancesRecord a payment to KRA▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min

Description

Record it after paying KRA. Every charge in the period that is not yet in a remittance is attached to this one, and amount_due is their total. amount_paid is what you actually paid; difference is paid minus due. Attached charges are no longer counted in excise_duty_payable, and their excise duty can no longer be given back on a refund. A remittance is never edited or deleted: void a wrong one and record it again.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
period_startdate (Y-m-d)RequiredFirst day covered, today or earlier. Usually the 1st of the month
period_enddate (Y-m-d)RequiredLast day covered, not before period_start and not in the future
amount_paidnumericRequiredKES paid to KRA, greater than 0
kra_referencestringRequiredUp to 100 characters, for example the payment registration number. Can be used by only one active remittance; reusable once that one is voided
paid_atdate (Y-m-d)OptionalWhen it was paid, today or earlier. Default: now. Cash flow shows the payment on this date

Request

    "paid_at": "2026-09-18T00:00:00+03:00", "status": "active", "recorded_by": "api_key:4",

GET/api/v1/finance/excise-duty/remittancesList payments to KRA▶
API Key20/min

    "meta": {},

      "active": {"remittances": 1, "amount_due": 15.00, "amount_paid": 15.00},
      "voided": {"remittances": 0, "amount_due": 0.00, "amount_paid": 0.00},
      "payable": 3.00
    },
    "items": [
      {"id": 3, "period_start": "2026-08-01", "period_end": "2026-08-31", "charges": 2, "amount_due": 15.00, "amount_paid": 15.00, "difference": 0.00, "kra_reference": "PRN2026080001", "paid_at": "2026-09-18T00:00:00+03:00", "status": "active", "recorded_by": "api_key:4", "created_at": "2026-09-18T09:00:00+03:00", "voided_at": null, "voided_by": null, "void_reason": null}
    ],
    "pagination": {"page": 1, "per_page": 50, "total": 1, "last_page": 1}
  }
}
Winnings tax is withheld when a house-banked game (sportsbook, crash) settles a wager as won. The rate is set per product (winnings_tax_rate on PUT /products/{code}, 0 until the accountant sets it) and the base is the profit: payout plus bonus less the cash stake, or the whole payout and bonus for a free bet. Every taxed settlement writes a charge row in the same transaction as its winnings_tax ledger entry. The tax is not revenue: it is held as winnings_tax_payable on the balance sheet until you pay KRA and record a remittance. Returns and payment are due by the 20th of the following month (FINANCE_WINNINGS_TAX_FILING_DAY; confirm with your accountant).
A wager reversal is never refused, so tax can be given back after it was paid to KRA. A reversal writes a reversal row with a negative amount (a credit). Credits are never attached to a remittance that was already filed: the next remittance takes every unremitted row in its period plus every earlier unremitted credit, so the over-payment comes off the next return. What is owed is the plain sum of every row not yet remitted, and can be negative while credits exceed charges. The summary and charges list follow exclude_test; the payable amount, monthly returns and remittances always cover every customer. All GET endpoints are rate-limited to 20 requests/minute.

GET/api/v1/finance/winnings-tax/returnsMonthly returns: due, paid, outstanding and due date▶
API Key20/min

Description

One row per calendar month the range touches, covering the whole month and every customer. tax_due is charged minus reversed in the month, tax_remitted the part of the month's rows already in a recorded remittance (a credit taken by a later remittance counts there too), and outstanding what is left; it is negative for a credit not yet used. amount_to_remit is what a remittance for the whole month would record now: the month's unremitted rows plus every earlier unremitted credit, or 0 when credits cover it (such a remittance is refused). overdue is true once the due date has passed with amount_to_remit above 0, so a month the credits cover is never overdue.

POST/api/v1/finance/winnings-tax/remittancesRecord a payment to KRA▶
API KeyIdempotent30/min

Description

Record it after paying KRA. Every row in the period not yet in a remittance is attached to this one, plus every earlier credit not yet used, and amount_due is their net. A period whose net is zero or less is refused (422) and its credits wait for a later return. difference is paid minus due. A remittance is never edited or deleted: void a wrong one and record it again.

Error Responses

// 422 - nothing owed in the period

// 422 - e.g. future period, amount 0, or KRA reference already used

POST/api/v1/finance/winnings-tax/remittances/{encryptedIdentifier}/voidVoid a remittance with a reason▶
API KeyEncrypted IDIdempotent30/min

Description

The remittance stays on record, flagged as voided with who and when. Its rows, charges and credits, are detached and count as owed again.

Request Body

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
reasonstringRequired3 to 255 characters

Error Responses

// 404
{"success": false, "message": "Remittance not found"}

// 409 - already voided
{"success": false, "message": "Remittance is already voided"}

GET/api/v1/finance/export/{report}Download a report as CSV▶
API Key20/min

Description

Streams the report as a CSV file. It takes the same range and filters as the report itself and exports every row in the range, ignoring page and per_page. The file is UTF-8 with a byte order mark so Excel reads names correctly, named finance-<report>-<from>-<to>.csv. Phone numbers are masked. Text a spreadsheet would run as a formula (starting with =, +, - or @) is prefixed with an apostrophe so it stays text. Nested values such as ledger metadata are written as JSON in one cell. The range limit of 366 days applies, and very large exports take a while.

Reports

{report}Same asRows
ledger/finance/ledgerOne per ledger entry
deposits/finance/depositsOne per payment
withdrawals/finance/withdrawalsOne per payout
purchases/finance/purchasesOne per purchase
adjustments/finance/adjustmentsOne per manual adjustment
games/finance/gamesOne per game
competitions/finance/competitionsOne per competition
customers-top/finance/customers/topOne per customer (default range: last 30 days)
cash-flow/finance/cash-flowOne per period
income-statement/finance/income-statementOne per period (includes expenses and net income)
income-by-product/finance/income-statement (by_product)One per product, then a platform row
house-banked/finance/house-banked (by_product)One per house-banked product
trial-balance/finance/trial-balanceOne per account and entry type
expenses/finance/expensesOne per expense
taxes/finance/taxesOne per tax
excise-duty/finance/excise-dutyOne per period
excise-duty-charges/finance/excise-duty/chargesOne per charge (deposit, signup bonus or shop load; see the source and bet_shop_load_id columns)
excise-duty-returns/finance/excise-duty/returnsOne per month
excise-duty-remittances/finance/excise-duty/remittancesOne per KRA payment
disputes/finance/disputesOne per complaint
referral-bonuses/finance/referrals/bonusesOne per bonus
referral-withdrawals/finance/referrals/withdrawalsOne per referral withdrawal
promotions/finance/promotionsOne per signup bonus
house-funding/finance/house-fundingOne per house funding
bet-shops/finance/bet-shopsOne per bet shop
bet-shop-loads/finance/bet-shops/loadsOne per cash load
bet-shop-withdrawals/finance/bet-shops/withdrawalsOne per shop cash withdrawal
open-wallets/finance/open-walletsOne per untouched open wallet, as of now (participants as JSON)

The cash-flow export has house_funding and shop_float columns after unmatched, excise_withheld and excise_remitted columns before net_cash, and referral_paid, referral_pending, referral_failed, deposit_refunded, shop_float_redeemed, shop_cash_loaded, shop_cash_paid_out, winnings_tax_withheld and winnings_tax_remitted after it. The income-statement export ends with referral_payouts, promotions, house_banked, wager_promotions and winnings_tax_withheld. income-by-product has one row per product (product, game, revenue_model, revenue, promotions, winnings_tax_withheld, net) and a last platform row whose promotions also holds referral payouts and expenses. The ledger, adjustments, customers-top, trial-balance, income-statement and income-by-product exports take product and game.

The customer statement cannot be exported yet.

Error Responses

// 404 - unknown report name
{"success": false, "message": "Unknown report. Choose one of: ledger, deposits, ..."}

// 422 - bad parameters, e.g. range over 366 days

Response formats

Errors are JSON. Most have a message; newer endpoints also have success: false and sometimes a machine-readable code. A few older endpoints put the message in status or error instead; each endpoint shows its own bodies. Branch on the HTTP status and code, not on message text.

400 Bad Request

The request was understood but refused. The identifier in the path could not be decrypted:

{"message": "Invalid identifier."}

An Idempotency-Key longer than 64 characters:

{"message": "Idempotency-Key must be 64 characters or fewer"}

Business rules, for example:

{"message": "Insufficient balance"}

401 Unauthorized

Missing, unknown or deactivated X-API-KEY:

{"message": "Invalid or missing API key"}

404 Not Found

The record does not exist; each endpoint names it:

{"message": "Customer not found"}

An unknown URL:

{"message": "The route api/v1/customer could not be found."}

405 Method Not Allowed

{"message": "The POST method is not supported for route api/v1/customers/search. Supported methods: GET, HEAD."}

409 Conflict

The same Idempotency-Key was sent with a different body:

{"message": "Idempotency-Key reused with different request body"}

Or the action conflicts with the record's state; these carry a code:

{"success": false, "code": "already_resolved", "message": "Only unmatched deposits can be refunded", "data": {...}}

422 Validation Error

Returned when the request has Accept: application/json. errors lists every failing field:

{
  "message": "The email has already been taken. (and 1 more error)",
  "errors": {
    "email": ["The email has already been taken."],
    "account_no": ["The account no has already been taken."]
  }
}

Some older endpoints catch validation failures and return them as 500 (or, for withdrawals, 422 with the errors in status). Each endpoint says so where it happens.

429 Too Many Requests

Over a rate limit (see Rate Limits). Wait for the number of seconds in the Retry-After header.

{"message": "Too Many Attempts."}

500 Server Error

An unexpected failure. The body does not include details in production:

{"message": "Server Error"}

Endpoints that catch their own errors return their own body (for example {"status": "Error creating Customer"}). Retrying a write is safe with the same Idempotency-Key.
